Loot table — new `lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts`\n\nMirror `lib/game/mobLoot.ts` exactly (`{itemId,min,max,chance?}` entries, `clampUnit` roll loop).\n\n```ts\nexport type LootEntry = { itemId: string; min: number; max: number; chance?: number };\nexport type LootTier = \"common\" | \"rare\";\nexport const DUNGEON_LOOT: Record<LootTier, LootEntry[]>;\nexport function seededRng(seed: number): () => number;          // small mulberry32\nexport function rollDungeonLoot(rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }>;\n```\n\n- **common** (staples): `bread`, `cooked_chicken`, `bone`, `string`, `sliver_ore`/`gold_ore`, `seeds`, occasional `stone_pickaxe`.\n- **rare** (the payoff, low chances): `diamond_ore`/`sapphire_ore`, `ruby_sword`/`sapphire_sword`, `chestplate`/`helmet`, rare `diamond_pickaxe` — plus a common sub-roll so rare chests still carry staples.\n- `rollDungeonLoot` rolls tier first (`rng() < 0.25` → rare), then iterates that table.\n- All `itemId`s must exist in `ITEM_DEFS` (integrity-tested).\n\n### 3. Worldgen — `lib/world/generation.ts`\n\nMirror `placeStructures`/`placeHouse`. Add `GEN.dungeonCount` (~28).\n\n- `placeDungeons(world, rand)`: loop `dungeonCount`, pick `(cx, cz)`, pick an underground `floorY`, call `placeDungeon`.\n- `placeDungeon`: hollow cobblestone box, interior ~5×4×5, solid floor at `floorY-1`, mossy-cobble speckle (hash-gated like ore specks), 1–2 chests against a wall (`BlockId.Chest`), spawner block centered on floor (`BlockId.Spawner`).\n- **Validation must read only pure-from-seed terrain** (`getBiome`, center-distance, `highestSolidY`) — never blocks the dungeon or player mutates — so the load-time index re-derivation matches. Reject if too shallow (pokes surface), too deep (below ~y=6), or within ~40 of the map center (player spawn → no free starter loot).\n- Integrate **last** in `generateWorld` (after `placeStructures`).\n- **Use a dedicated dungeon LCG seeded from `world.seed`** (not the shared `rand()` stream) so the index sets can be re-derived on load without replaying the whole pipeline.\n- `collectDungeonSites(world): { chestIndices: number[]; spawnerIndices: number[] }` — re-runs the same placement math in **derive mode** (collect indices, no block writes), recreating the dungeon LCG from `world.seed`. Cost is negligible (~28 iterations). This is the session-derived index source for §5.\n\n**Risk to control:** `collectDungeonSites` must agree exactly with `placeDungeons`. Mitigated by terrain-only validation + a test asserting derived indices == actual `Chest`/`Spawner` positions in the generated world.\n\n### 4. Day-independent (dungeons are dark).\n- Respects global `HOSTILE_CAP` (shared `state.mobs`) **and** a per-spawner `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP`.\n- Config tunables: `SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (~8), `SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS` (~16), `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP` (~6). Add `spawnerTimer` to `GameTimers` + `createTimers()`.\n- Step order in `GameEngine.step`: insert after `tickHostileSpawnDirector`, before `tickMobs`.\n- Juice: new `GameEvent` `{ type: \"mobSpawned\"; kind; x; y; z }` → route a soft poof in `audioDirector.ts handleEvent` and a dark smoke burst in `GameRenderer.handleEvent` (mirror the `mobDied` burst).\n\n### 5. Verify the meshing geometry hash only shifts if a dungeon lands in the test region (underground → likely unaffected; check).\n- New: **dungeon presence probe** (count underground `Chest`/`Spawner`/`MossyCobblestone` for seed 1337, assert > 0; assert no dungeon chest near spawn center).\n- New: **`collectDungeonSites` agreement** test (derived indices == actual block positions) — guards the exploit fix.\n- New `lib/game/dungeonLoot.test.ts`: every `itemId` ∈ `ITEM_DEFS`; min/max-roll determinism; same-seed reproducibility.\n- `lib/game/save.test.ts`: v4→v5 migration round-trip; `lootedChests` serialize/read; corrupt input → `[]`.\n- `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts` (small world has no dungeons → inject a chest + add idx to `dungeonChestIndices` manually):\n  - **loot-persistence round-trip / exploit-closed**: open → loot present → empty it → `serialize()` → new engine from save → reopen → empty, no re-roll.\n  - first open fills, second open (same session) doesn't.\n  - fill-on-break yields loot.\n  - **spawner tick**: place spawner, player near, run an interval → hostile count rises; assert capped.\n\n## Docs & changelog\n\n- `docs/save-format.md` — add v5 (`lootedChests`); note `SAVE_KEY` → v6 and *why* (worldgen baseline shift); correct the v4 \"chests never generated\" claim.\n- `docs/architecture.md` — `placeDungeons` in the pipeline; lazy dungeon-loot + session/persisted Sets in the block-entity narrative; `tickSpawnerDirector` in the step order.\n- `docs/reference.md` — new blocks, loot tiers, new tunables, save v5.\n- `docs/manual.md` — player guide: explore underground for dungeons/loot, beware spawners.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — `dungeonCount`, `SPAWNER_*`, loot chances.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — add \"a worldgen structure\" + \"a loot table / lazy block-entity fill\" recipes (reusable patterns).\n- `docs/testing.md` — dungeon coverage rows; > AGENT

The plan is written. Here's the summary:

## Dungeons & Loot Structures (v0.7.0)

**The fun:** small underground cobblestone rooms scattered through the world — each with 1–2 **tiered loot chests** (a real reason to explore and dig) and a **mob spawner** that drip-spawns hostiles when you get close (real underground tension). Plus mossy-cobble flavor. All zero-asset, building on the chest/block-entity layer you just shipped.

**The one genuinely tricky part** — and why this needed a real design pass: dungeon chests are placed by *deterministic worldgen*, so their contents aren't in the save baseline. Pre-filling them naively would re-roll fresh loot on every reload → **infinite-loot exploit**. The fix is a three-part lifecycle: a session-derived Set of dungeon-chest indices (recomputed from the seed), a persisted "looted" Set (new save field), and lazy loot-fill on first open — marked looted *on open*, not on empty, so a chest never re-rolls once touched.

**Scope confirmed with you:** full slice (rooms + loot + spawners), and world reset is fine — `SAVE_KEY` bumps v5→v6 and the worldgen hashes get re-baselined per the documented policy.

**6 bisectable commits**, with the only hash-touching change isolated to commit 3 (the worldgen one) for a clean review/revert point. Tests cover the exploit-closed round-trip, the `collectDungeonSites` agreement (the key correctness risk), loot determinism, and the spawner tick. Docs span all 7 `docs/` pages plus the changelog and version bump.

Reject if too shallow (pokes surface), too deep (below ~y=6), or within ~40 of the map center (player spawn → no free starter loot).\n- Integrate **last** in `generateWorld` (after `placeStructures`).\n- **Use a dedicated dungeon LCG seeded from `world.seed`** (not the shared `rand()` stream) so the index sets can be re-derived on load without replaying the whole pipeline.\n- `collectDungeonSites(world): { chestIndices: number[]; spawnerIndices: number[] }` — re-runs the same placement math in **derive mode** (collect indices, no block writes), recreating the dungeon LCG from `world.seed`. Cost is negligible (~28 iterations). This is the session-derived index source for §5.\n\n**Risk to control:** `collectDungeonSites` must agree exactly with `placeDungeons`. Mitigated by terrain-only validation + a test asserting derived indices == actual `Chest`/`Spawner` positions in the generated world.\n\n### 4. Mob spawner — `lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts`, `config.ts`, `state.ts`, `GameEngine.ts`\n\n- New `tickSpawnerDirector(state, dt, rng, surfaceYAt, emit)`: on a timer, for each `dungeonSpawnerIndices` entry, if player within `SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS`, the block is still a `Spawner`, and counts are under cap, spawn 1 hostile **at the spawner's own floor y** (not surface — needs an explicit-y spawn; spawn at room-center floor + small jitter, room is hollow so it's safe). Day-independent (dungeons are dark).\n- Respects global `HOSTILE_CAP` (shared `state.mobs`) **and** a per-spawner `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP`.\n- Config tunables: `SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (~8), `SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS` (~16), `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP` (~6). Add `spawnerTimer` to `GameTimers` + `createTimers()`.\n- Step order in `GameEngine.step`: insert after `tickHostileSpawnDirector`, before `tickMobs`.\n- Juice: new `GameEvent` `{ type: \"mobSpawned\"; kind; x; y; z }` → route a soft poof in `audioDirector.ts handleEvent` and a dark smoke burst in `GameRenderer.handleEvent` (mirror the `mobDied` burst).\n\n### 5. Loot persistence — `lib/game/types.ts`, `save.ts`, `state.ts`, `GameEngine.ts`, `interact.ts`, `mining.ts`\n\n- **`state.ts` `GameState`**: add `dungeonChestIndices: Set<number>` (session), `dungeonSpawnerIndices: Set<number>` (session), `lootedDungeonChests: Set<number>` (persisted).\n- **Save format v5** (`types.ts`): rename current `SaveData` → `SaveDataV4`; new `SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV4,\"version\"> & { version: 5; lootedChests?: number[] }`.\n- **`save.ts`**: `migrateSaveV4toV5` (pure version bump); chain it in `readSave`; `serializeLootedChests(set)` / `readLootedChests(save)` (validate finite numbers).\n- **`GameEngine` ctor**: after `generateWorld`, `state.dungeonChestIndices`/`dungeonSpawnerIndices` ← `collectDungeonSites(world)`; if loading a save, `state.lootedDungeonChests` ← `readLootedChests(save)`. `serialize()` writes `version: 5` + `lootedChests`.\n- **`interact.ts` `interactChest`**: before creating the empty store, if `dungeonChestIndices.has(idx) && !lootedDungeonChests.has(idx)`, fill via a shared `fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted(state, idx)` helper (seeds `seededRng((world.seed ^ idx ^ 0x9e3779b1) >>> 0)`, rolls `rollDungeonLoot`, inserts with `tryInsertSlots`/`createSlot`). **Mark `idx` looted on open** (not on empty) — this is the exploit fix.\n- **`mining.ts`**: in the chest-break path, call the same `fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted` before spilling, so breaking an unopened dungeon chest still yields its loot. Mark looted there too.\n\n## Critical files\n\n- `lib/world/generation.ts` — `placeDungeons`/`placeDungeon`/`collectDungeonSites`, `GEN.dungeonCount`, dungeon LCG\n- `lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts` (new) — tiered table, `rollDungeonLoot`, `seededRng`\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts` — lazy loot-fill in `interactChest` + `fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted` helper\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts` — fill-on-break\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts` — `tickSpawnerDirector` + explicit-y spawn\n- `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` — site derivation, looted-set restore, serialize, step-order wire\n- `lib/game/save.ts` + `lib/game/types.ts` — v5 migration, looted-set serialize/read\n- `lib/game/engine/state.ts` — new `GameState` Set fields, `mobSpawned` event, `spawnerTimer`\n- `lib/world/blocks.ts`, `lib/game/items.ts`, `lib/game/audio/materials.ts`, `lib/world/atlas.ts` — new blocks\n- `lib/game/config.ts` — `SAVE_KEY` → v6, spawner tunables\n\n## Tests (per docs/testing.md)\n\n- **Re-baseline** all worldgen SHA digests in `lib/world/generation.test.ts` (3 small-world seeds + full 512 world) — deliberate change, log/paste new hashes in the same commit. Verify the meshing geometry hash only shifts if a dungeon lands in the test region (underground → likely unaffected; check).\n- New: **dungeon presence probe** (count underground `Chest`/`Spawner`/`MossyCobblestone` for seed 1337, assert > 0; assert no dungeon chest near spawn center).\n- New: **`collectDungeonSites` agreement** test (derived indices == actual block positions) — guards the exploit fix.\n- New `lib/game/dungeonLoot.test.ts`: every `itemId` ∈ `ITEM_DEFS`; min/max-roll determinism; same-seed reproducibility.\n- `lib/game/save.test.ts`: v4→v5 migration round-trip; `lootedChests` serialize/read; corrupt input → `[]`.\n- `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts` (small world has no dungeons → inject a chest + add idx to `dungeonChestIndices` manually):\n  - **loot-persistence round-trip / exploit-closed**: open → loot present → empty it → `serialize()` → new engine from save → reopen → empty, no re-roll.\n  - first open fills, second open (same session) doesn't.\n  - fill-on-break yields loot.\n  - **spawner tick**: place spawner, player near, run an interval → hostile count rises; assert capped.\n\n## Docs & changelog\n\n- `docs/save-format.md` — add v5 (`lootedChests`); note `SAVE_KEY` → v6 and *why* (worldgen baseline shift); correct the v4 \"chests never generated\" claim.\n- `docs/architecture.md` — `placeDungeons` in the pipeline; lazy dungeon-loot + session/persisted Sets in the block-entity narrative; `tickSpawnerDirector` in the step order.\n- `docs/reference.md` — new blocks, loot tiers, new tunables, save v5.\n- `docs/manual.md` — player guide: explore underground for dungeons/loot, beware spawners.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — `dungeonCount`, `SPAWNER_*`, loot chances.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — add \"a worldgen structure\" + \"a loot table / lazy block-entity fill\" recipes (reusable patterns).\n- `docs/testing.md` — dungeon coverage rows; 1	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
```
2	
3	/**
4	 * A single loot entry: yields a uniformly random count in [min, max], gated by
5	 * an optional drop chance (default: always). Counts are inclusive on both ends.
6	 */
7	export type MobDrop = {
8	  itemId: string;
9	  min: number;
10	  max: number;
11	  /** 0..1 probability the entry drops at all; omitted means always. */
12	  chance?: number;
13	};
14	
15	/**
16	 * What each mob drops when it dies. Hostiles yield crafting/combat materials,
17	 * passives yield materials plus raw meat. Item ids must exist in ITEM_DEFS —
18	 * mobLoot.test.ts enforces that.
19	 */
20	export const MOB_DROPS: Record<MobKind, MobDrop[]> = {
21	  sheep: [
22	    { itemId: "wool", min: 1, max: 2 },
23	    { itemId: "raw_mutton", min: 1, max: 1 }
24	  ],
25	  chicken: [
26	    { itemId: "feather", min: 0, max: 2 },
27	    { itemId: "raw_chicken", min: 1, max: 1 }
28	  ],
29	  horse: [{ itemId: "leather", min: 1, max: 2 }],
30	  zombie: [{ itemId: "rotten_flesh", min: 1, max: 2 }],
31	  skeleton: [{ itemId: "bone", min: 1, max: 2 }],
32	  spider: [{ itemId: "string", min: 0, max: 2 }]
33	};
34	
35	/** Clamps an rng sample into [0, 1) so a pathological injected rng can't over-roll. */
36	const clampUnit = (v: number): number => Math.min(1 - Number.EPSILON, Math.max(0, v));
37	
38	/**
39	 * Rolls the drop table for a mob kind. Returns one entry per item that yielded a
40	 * positive count; the engine adds each to the inventory. `rng` is injectable so
41	 * tests get deterministic counts (0 → min, ~1 → max).
42	 */
43	export function rollMobDrops(kind: MobKind, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
44	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
45	  for (const entry of MOB_DROPS[kind]) {
46	    if (entry.chance !== undefined && clampUnit(rng()) >= entry.chance) continue;
47	    const span = entry.max - entry.min + 1;
48	    const count = entry.min + Math.floor(clampUnit(rng()) * span);
49	    if (count > 0) drops.push({ itemId: entry.itemId, count });
50	  }
51	  return drops;
52	}
53
```

```
116	export const ITEM_DEF_BY_ID: Record<string, ItemDef> = Object.fromEntries(ITEM_DEFS.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
117	
118	export function createEmptySlot(): InventorySlot {
119	  return { id: null, label: "Empty", kind: null, count: 0 };
120	}
121	
122	export function createSlot(itemId: string, count: number): InventorySlot {
123	  const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId];
124	  if (!def) return createEmptySlot();
125	  const slot: InventorySlot = { ...def, count };
126	  if ((def.kind === "tool" || def.kind === "weapon" || def.kind === "armor") && def.maxDurability) {
127	    slot.maxDurability = def.maxDurability;
128	    slot.durability = def.maxDurability;
129	  }
130	  return slot;
131	}
132	
133	export function createInitialInventory(): InventorySlot[] {
134	  const slots: InventorySlot[] = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
135	  const starter: Array<{ id: string; count: number }> = [
136	    { id: "grass", count: 64 },
137	    { id: "dirt", count: 64 },
138	    { id: "stone", count: 64 },
139	    { id: "wood", count: 64 },
140	    { id: "planks", count: 20 },
141	    { id: "cobble", count: 20 },
142	    { id: "sand", count: 20 },
143	    { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1 },
144	    { id: "knife", count: 1 }
145	  ];
146	  for (let i = 0; i < starter.length && i < slots.length; i += 1) slots[i] = createSlot(starter[i].id, starter[i].count);
147	  return slots;
148	}
149	
150	export const BLOCK_TO_SLOT: Partial<Record<BlockId, string>> = {
151	  [BlockId.Grass]: "grass",
152	  [BlockId.Dirt]: "dirt",
153	  [BlockId.Stone]: "stone",
154	  [BlockId.Wood]: "wood",
155	  [BlockId.Leaves]: "dirt",
156	  [BlockId.Planks]: "planks",
157	  [BlockId.Cobblestone]: "cobble",
158	  [BlockId.Sand]: "sand",
159	  [BlockId.Brick]: "brick",
160	  [BlockId.Glass]: "glass",
161	  [BlockId.SliverOre]: "sliver_ore",
162	  [BlockId.RubyOre]: "ruby_ore",
163	  [BlockId.GoldOre]: "gold_ore",
164	  [BlockId.SapphireOre]: "sapphire_ore",
165	  [BlockId.DiamondOre]: "diamond_ore",
166	  [BlockId.Snow]: "snow",
167	  [BlockId.Cactus]: "cactus",
168	  [BlockId.Bed]: "bed",
169	  [BlockId.Furnace]: "furnace",
170	  [BlockId.Chest]: "chest",
171	  // Tilled soil reverts to dirt; immature wheat returns its seed.
172	  [BlockId.Farmland]: "dirt",
173	  [BlockId.WheatStage0]: "seeds",
174	  [BlockId.WheatStage1]: "seeds",
175	  [BlockId.WheatStage2]: "seeds"
176	};
177	
178	/**
179	 * Items a broken block yields. The default is its single `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` entry;
180	 * grass occasionally also drops a seed (the natural seed source), and mature
181	 * wheat drops wheat plus 1–2 seeds. `rng` is injectable for deterministic tests.
182	 */
183	export function rollBlockDrops(block: BlockId, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
184	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
185	  const base = BLOCK_TO_SLOT[block];
186	  if (base) drops.push({ itemId: base, count: 1 });
187	
188	  if (block === BlockId.Grass && rng() < GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE) {
189	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 });
190	  }
191	  if (block === BlockId.WheatStage3) {
192	    drops.push({ itemId: "wheat", count: 1 });
193	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 + Math.floor(rng() * 2) });
194	  }
195	  return drops;
196	}
197
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BLOCK_COLORS, BlockId } from "./blocks";
3	
4	// Procedural texture atlas: one 16x16 tile per block face variant, painted on a
5	// canvas at startup. This is the only world module that touches the DOM.
6	export const ATLAS_TILE_SIZE = 16;
7	export const ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS = 3; // top, side, bottom
8	export const ATLAS_COLUMNS = 16;
9	
10	// Tile range derived from the palette so new blocks get tiles automatically —
11	// a hardcoded last-block bound here once left new blocks sampling garbage UVs.
12	export const ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT = Math.max(...Object.keys(BLOCK_COLORS).map(Number)) + 1;
13	export const ATLAS_ROWS = Math.ceil((ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS) / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
14	
15	let atlasTextureCache: THREE.CanvasTexture | null = null;
16	
17	function clamp01(v: number): number {
18	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
19	}
20	
21	function tone(c: [number, number, number], mul: number, add = 0): [number, number, number] {
22	  return [clamp01(c[0] * mul + add), clamp01(c[1] * mul + add), clamp01(c[2] * mul + add)];
23	}
24	
25	function rgb(c: [number, number, number]): string {
26	  return `rgb(${Math.floor(clamp01(c[0]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[1]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[2]) * 255)})`;
27	}
28	
29	export function tileIndexFor(block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom"): number {
30	  const faceId = face === "top" ? 0 : face === "side" ? 1 : 2;
31	  return block * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS + faceId;
32	}
33	
34	export function createBlockAtlasTexture(): THREE.CanvasTexture {
35	  if (atlasTextureCache) return atlasTextureCache;
36	
37	  const width = ATLAS_COLUMNS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
38	  const height = ATLAS_ROWS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
39	  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
40	  canvas.width = width;
41	  canvas.height = height;
42	  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
43	  if (!ctx) throw new Error("Failed to create atlas context");
44	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
45	
46	  const drawTile = (block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom") => {
47	    const tile = tileIndexFor(block, face);
48	    const col = tile % ATLAS_COLUMNS;
49	    const row = Math.floor(tile / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
50	    const ox = col * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
51	    const oy = row * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
52	
53	    const baseBlockColor = BLOCK_COLORS[block] ?? [1, 0, 1];
54	    let base = baseBlockColor;
55	    if (face === "top") base = tone(base, 1.08);
56	    if (face === "bottom") base = tone(base, 0.96);
57	    if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "bottom") base = BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Dirt];
58	
59	    for (let y = 0; y < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; y += 1) {
60	      for (let x = 0; x < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; x += 1) {
61	        const h = Math.sin((x + block * 13 + (face === "top" ? 7 : face === "side" ? 17 : 29)) * 12.1 + (y + block * 19) * 7.7) * 43758.5453;
62	        const n = h - Math.floor(h);
63	        let c = tone(base, 0.92 + n * 0.22);
64	
65	        if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "side" && y < 4) c = tone(BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Grass], 0.95 + n * 0.15);
66	        if ((block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Bedrock) && n > 0.8) c = tone(base, 1.18);
67	        if ((block === BlockId.Wood || block === BlockId.Planks) && (x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82);
68	        if (block === BlockId.SliverOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.93, 0.93, 0.95], 1);
69	        if (block === BlockId.RubyOre && n > 0.88) c = tone([0.86, 0.24, 0.24], 1);
70	        if (block === BlockId.GoldOre && n > 0.84) c = tone([0.96, 0.8, 0.25], 1);
71	        if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.2, 0.62, 0.9], 1);
72	        if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre && n > 0.9) c = tone([0.7, 0.94, 0.98], 1);
73	        if (block === BlockId.Water) c = tone([0.22, 0.48, 0.85], 0.95 + n * 0.12, face === "top" ? 0.02 : 0);
74	        if (block === BlockId.Sand && n > 0.84) c = tone(base, 1.12);
75	        if (block === BlockId.Cactus && face === "side" && x % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.72);
76	        if (block === BlockId.Snow) c = tone(base, 0.97 + n * 0.06);
77	        if (block === BlockId.Bed && face === "top" && y < 5) c = tone([0.95, 0.95, 0.97], 0.95 + n * 0.1); // pillow band
78	        if (block >= BlockId.WheatStage0 && block <= BlockId.WheatStage3 && face === "side" && x % 3 === 1) c = tone(base, 0.66); // gaps read as stalks
79	        if (block === BlockId.Furnace && face === "side" && x >= 5 && x <= 10 && y >= 8 && y <= 12) c = tone([0.95, 0.45, 0.12], 0.85 + n * 0.3); // glowing mouth
80	        if (block === BlockId.Chest) {
81	          if ((x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82); // plank grain
82	          if (face === "top" && y === 8) c = tone(base, 0.6); // lid seam
83	          if (face === "side" && y === 7) c = tone(base, 0.58); // lid joint band
84	          if (face === "side" && x >= 7 && x <= 8 && y >= 6 && y <= 9) c = tone([0.62, 0.64, 0.68], 0.9 + n * 0.2); // metal latch
85	        }
86	
87	        ctx.fillStyle = rgb(c);
88	        ctx.fillRect(ox + x, oy + y, 1, 1);
89	      }
90	    }
91	  };
92	
93	  for (let block = BlockId.Grass; block < ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT; block += 1) {
94	    drawTile(block, "top");
95	    drawTile(block, "side");
96	    drawTile(block, "bottom");
97	  }
98	
99	  const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
100	  texture.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
101	  texture.minFilter = THREE.NearestMipmapNearestFilter;
102	  texture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
103	  texture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
104	  texture.flipY = false;
105	  texture.needsUpdate = true;
106	  atlasTextureCache = texture;
107	  return texture;
108	}
109
```

```
1	import { BiomeId, BlockId } from "./blocks";
2	import { VoxelWorld } from "./voxelWorld";
3	
4	/**
5	 * Deterministic terrain generation.
6	 *
7	 * ⚠ Saves store only the seed plus block-change deltas, so generateWorld() must
8	 * produce byte-identical output for a given seed forever. Generation order, the
9	 * PRNG, the noise functions, and every constant below are part of the save
10	 * format. generation.test.ts pins the output with SHA-256 digests — if it fails
11	 * after a change here, the change breaks existing saves.
12	 */
13	
14	/**
15	 * Every value here is part of the save format (see module header). Names exist
16	 * for readability; changing a value re-rolls every world.
17	 */
18	export const GEN = Object.freeze({
19	  seaLevel: 43,
20	  borderWallHeight: 14,
21	  biomes: Object.freeze({
22	    [BiomeId.Plains]: Object.freeze({ baseHeight: 47, noiseScale: 2.0, treeChance: 0.18 }),
23	    [BiomeId.Desert]: Object.freeze({ baseHeight: 46, noiseScale: 3.5, treeChance: 0.01 }),
24	    [BiomeId.Ocean]: Object.freeze({ baseHeight: 30, noiseScale: 2.5, treeChance: 0 }),
25	    [BiomeId.Forest]: Object.freeze({ baseHeight: 50, noiseScale: 4.0, treeChance: 0.6 }),
26	    [BiomeId.Mountains]: Object.freeze({ baseHeight: 55, noiseScale: 18.0, treeChance: 0.05 })
27	  }),
28	  mountainStoneAboveY: 65,
29	  snowAboveY: 68,
30	  // Sand shoreline band around sea level (deeper floors keep their biome top).
31	  beachMaxAboveSea: 1,
32	  beachDepthBelowSea: 2,
33	  caveCount: 230,
34	  caveChamberCount: 150,
35	  treeAttempts: 9000,
36	  // Cacti per dry desert column — keyed on area so drowned deserts get few.
37	  cactusChance: 0.012,
38	  houseCount: 120,
39	  oreConfigs: Object.freeze([
40	    { id: BlockId.SliverOre, attempts: 120000, minY: 3, maxYOffset: 10, minSize: 3, maxSize: 10 },
41	    { id: BlockId.RubyOre, attempts: 52000, minY: 2, maxYOffset: 16, minSize: 3, maxSize: 10 },
42	    { id: BlockId.GoldOre, attempts: 36000, minY: 2, maxYOffset: 22, minSize: 3, maxSize: 10 },
43	    { id: BlockId.SapphireOre, attempts: 28000, minY: 2, maxYOffset: 28, minSize: 2, maxSize: 7 },
44	    { id: BlockId.DiamondOre, attempts: 18000, minY: 2, maxYOffset: 36, minSize: 2, maxSize: 6 }
45	  ])
46	});
47	
48	function hash2D(x: number, z: number): number {
49	  const n = Math.sin(x * 127.1 + z * 311.7) * 43758.5453123;
50	  return n - Math.floor(n);
51	}
52	
53	function smoothNoise2D(x: number, z: number, seed: number): number {
54	  const s = seed * 0.013;
55	  const val =
56	    Math.sin(x * 0.015 + s) * 1.2 +
57	    Math.cos(z * 0.012 + s * 1.4) * 1.2 +
58	    Math.sin(x * 0.005 - z * 0.007 + s * 0.7) * 3.5 +
59	    Math.cos(x * 0.031 + z * 0.027 + s * 2.1) * 0.4;
60	  return val;
61	}
62	
63	export function generateWorld(world: VoxelWorld): void {
64	  const rand = (() => {
65	    let t = (world.seed >>> 0) + 0x6d2b79f5;
66	    return () => {
67	      t += 0x6d2b79f5;
68	      let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
69	      r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
70	      return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
71	    };
72	  })();
73	
74	  generateTerrain(world);
75	  carveCaves(world, rand);
76	  placeWater(world);
77	  placeBeaches(world);
78	  placeOres(world, rand);
79	  placeTrees(world, rand);
80	  placeCacti(world);
81	  placeStructures(world, rand);
82	}
83	
84	function generateTerrain(world: VoxelWorld): void {
85	  const maxX = world.sizeX - 1;
86	  const maxZ = world.sizeZ - 1;
87	
88	  // Fast clear and bedrock
89	  world.blocks.fill(BlockId.Air);
90	  for (let x = 0; x < world.sizeX; x += 1) {
91	    for (let z = 0; z < world.sizeZ; z += 1) {
92	      world.set(x, 0, z, BlockId.Bedrock);
93	    }
94	  }
95	
96	  for (let x = 0; x < world.sizeX; x += 1) {
97	    for (let z = 0; z < world.sizeZ; z += 1) {
98	      const biome = world.getBiome(x, z);
99	      const noise = smoothNoise2D(x, z, world.seed);
100	      const { baseHeight, noiseScale } = GEN.biomes[biome];
101	
102	      const topY = Math.max(5, Math.min(world.sizeY - 5, Math.floor(baseHeight + noise * noiseScale)));
103	      let topBlock = BlockId.Grass;
104	      if (biome === BiomeId.Desert || biome === BiomeId.Ocean) {
105	        topBlock = BlockId.Sand;
106	      } else if (biome === BiomeId.Mountains && topY > GEN.snowAboveY) {
107	        topBlock = BlockId.Snow;
108	      } else if (biome === BiomeId.Mountains && topY > GEN.mountainStoneAboveY) {
109	        topBlock = BlockId.Stone;
110	      }
111	
112	      for (let y = 1; y <= topY; y += 1) {
113	        if (y === topY) {
114	          world.set(x, y, z, topBlock);
115	        } else if (y > topY - 3) {
116	          world.set(x, y, z, topBlock === BlockId.Sand ? BlockId.Sand : BlockId.Dirt);
117	        } else if (y > topY - 8 && hash2D(x * 0.2 + y * 0.1, z * 0.2) > 0.88) {
118	          world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Cobblestone);
119	        } else {
120	          world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Stone);
121	        }
122	      }
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  // Unbreakable border walls.
127	  for (let y = 1; y <= GEN.borderWallHeight; y += 1) {
128	    for (let x = 0; x < world.sizeX; x += 1) {
129	      world.set(x, y, 0, BlockId.Bedrock);
130	      world.set(x, y, maxZ, BlockId.Bedrock);
131	    }
132	    for (let z = 0; z < world.sizeZ; z += 1) {
133	      world.set(0, y, z, BlockId.Bedrock);
134	      world.set(maxX, y, z, BlockId.Bedrock);
135	    }
136	  }
137	}
138	
139	function carveCaves(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
140	  const carveSphere = (cx: number, cy: number, cz: number, radius: number) => {
141	    const r2 = radius * radius;
142	    const minX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cx - radius));
143	    const maxXc = Math.min(world.sizeX - 2, Math.ceil(cx + radius));
144	    const minY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cy - radius));
145	    const maxYc = Math.min(world.sizeY - 2, Math.ceil(cy + radius));
146	    const minZ = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cz - radius));
147	    const maxZc = Math.min(world.sizeZ - 2, Math.ceil(cz + radius));
148	    for (let y = minY; y <= maxYc; y += 1) {
149	      for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZc; z += 1) {
150	        for (let x = minX; x <= maxXc; x += 1) {
151	          const dx = x - cx;
152	          const dy = y - cy;
153	          const dz = z - cz;
154	          if (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > r2) continue;
155	          const block = world.get(x, y, z);
156	          if (block !== BlockId.Bedrock) world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Air);
157	        }
158	      }
159	    }
160	  };
161	
162	  for (let i = 0; i < GEN.caveCount; i += 1) {
163	    let x = 12 + rand() * (world.sizeX - 24);
164	    let y = 3 + rand() * (world.sizeY - 9);
165	    let z = 12 + rand() * (world.sizeZ - 24);
166	    let yaw = rand() * Math.PI * 2;
167	    let pitch = (rand() - 0.5) * 0.26;
168	    const length = 56 + Math.floor(rand() * 56);
169	    for (let step = 0; step < length; step += 1) {
170	      const r = 1.9 + rand() * 2.6;
171	      carveSphere(x, y, z, r);
172	      if (rand() > 0.968) carveSphere(x, y, z, 4.8 + rand() * 4.6);
173	      yaw += (rand() - 0.5) * 0.28;
174	      pitch = Math.max(-0.55, Math.min(0.55, pitch + (rand() - 0.5) * 0.16));
175	      x += Math.cos(yaw);
176	      z += Math.sin(yaw);
177	      y += Math.sin(pitch) * 0.8;
178	      if (x < 8 || x > world.sizeX - 8 || z < 8 || z > world.sizeZ - 8 || y < 2 || y > world.sizeY - 4) break;
179	    }
180	  }
181	
182	  for (let i = 0; i < GEN.caveChamberCount; i += 1) {
183	    const cx = 12 + rand() * (world.sizeX - 24);
184	    const cy = 5 + rand() * (world.sizeY - 14);
185	    const cz = 12 + rand() * (world.sizeZ - 24);
186	    carveSphere(cx, cy, cz, 5.4 + rand() * 6.4);
187	  }
188	}
189	
190	function placeWater(world: VoxelWorld): void {
191	  const { seaLevel } = GEN;
192	  for (let x = 1; x < world.sizeX - 1; x += 1) {
193	    for (let z = 1; z < world.sizeZ - 1; z += 1) {
194	      const topY = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
195	      if (topY >= seaLevel) continue;
196	      for (let y = topY + 1; y <= seaLevel; y += 1) {
197	        if (world.get(x, y, z) === BlockId.Air) world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Water);
198	      }
199	    }
200	  }
201	}
202	
203	function placeOres(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
204	  const hasNearbyAir = (x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean => {
205	    return (
206	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
207	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
208	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
209	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
210	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Air ||
211	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Air ||
212	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
213	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
214	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
215	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
216	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Water ||
217	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Water
218	    );
219	  };
220	
221	  const placeOreVein = (x: number, y: number, z: number, ore: BlockId, minSize: number, maxSize: number) => {
222	    const size = minSize + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, maxSize - minSize + 1));
223	    for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) {
224	      const vx = x + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
225	      const vy = y + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 3);
226	      const vz = z + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
227	      if (!world.inBounds(vx, vy, vz) || vy <= 1) continue;
228	      const b = world.get(vx, vy, vz);
229	      if (b === BlockId.Stone || b === BlockId.Cobblestone) world.set(vx, vy, vz, ore);
230	    }
231	  };
232	
233	  for (const config of GEN.oreConfigs) {
234	    for (let i = 0; i < config.attempts; i += 1) {
235	      const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));
236	      const y = config.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - config.maxYOffset));
237	      const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));
238	      const block = world.get(x, y, z);
239	      if ((block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) || !hasNearbyAir(x, y, z)) continue;
240	      placeOreVein(x, y, z, config.id, config.minSize, config.maxSize);
241	    }
242	  }
243	}
244	
245	/**
246	 * Sand shorelines: grass columns in the sea-level band turn to beach only
247	 * when water actually sits nearby — inland basins at sea level stay grass.
248	 * Runs after placeWater so adjacency can test real water blocks.
249	 */
250	function placeBeaches(world: VoxelWorld): void {
251	  for (let x = 1; x < world.sizeX - 1; x += 1) {
252	    for (let z = 1; z < world.sizeZ - 1; z += 1) {
253	      const topY = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
254	      if (topY > GEN.seaLevel + GEN.beachMaxAboveSea || topY < GEN.seaLevel - GEN.beachDepthBelowSea) continue;
255	      if (world.get(x, topY, z) !== BlockId.Grass) continue;
256	
257	      let nearWater = false;
258	      for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2 && !nearWater; dx += 1) {
259	        for (let dz = -2; dz <= 2 && !nearWater; dz += 1) {
260	          if (world.get(x + dx, GEN.seaLevel, z + dz) === BlockId.Water) nearWater = true;
261	        }
262	      }
263	      if (!nearWater) continue;
264	
265	      for (let y = Math.max(1, topY - 2); y <= topY; y += 1) world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Sand);
266	    }
267	  }
268	}
269	
270	function placeTrees(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
271	  for (let i = 0; i < GEN.treeAttempts; i += 1) {
272	    const x = 4 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 8));
273	    const z = 4 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 8));
274	
275	    const biome = world.getBiome(x, z);
276	    const spawnChance = GEN.biomes[biome].treeChance;
277	
278	    if (rand() > spawnChance) continue;
279	
280	    const topY = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
281	    if (world.get(x, topY, z) !== BlockId.Grass) continue;
282	
283	    const trunkHeight = spawnChance > 0.5 ? 4 + Math.floor(rand() * 3) : 3 + Math.floor(rand() * 3);
284	    for (let y = 1; y <= trunkHeight; y += 1) world.set(x, topY + y, z, BlockId.Wood);
285	
286	    const leafStart = topY + trunkHeight - 1;
287	    for (let ox = -2; ox <= 2; ox += 1) {
288	      for (let oz = -2; oz <= 2; oz += 1) {
289	        for (let oy = 0; oy <= 2; oy += 1) {
290	          const d = Math.abs(ox) + Math.abs(oz) + oy;
291	          if (d > 4) continue;
292	          // Only fill air: the canopy must not eat the trunk or neighbors.
293	          if (world.get(x + ox, leafStart + oy, z + oz) !== BlockId.Air) continue;
294	          world.set(x + ox, leafStart + oy, z + oz, BlockId.Leaves);
295	        }
296	      }
297	    }
298	  }
299	}
300	
301	function placeCacti(world: VoxelWorld): void {
302	  // Hash-gated scan instead of random attempts: density follows the dry
303	  // desert area, so a mostly-flooded desert gets few cacti instead of the
304	  // whole budget missing. Doesn't consume the shared rand stream.
305	  for (let x = 4; x < world.sizeX - 4; x += 1) {
306	    for (let z = 4; z < world.sizeZ - 4; z += 1) {
307	      if (hash2D(x * 1.7 + 11.3, z * 2.3 - 7.1) > GEN.cactusChance) continue;
308	      if (world.getBiome(x, z) !== BiomeId.Desert) continue;
309	
310	      const topY = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
311	      if (world.get(x, topY, z) !== BlockId.Sand) continue;
312	      if (world.get(x, topY + 1, z) !== BlockId.Air) continue;
313	
314	      const height = 2 + (hash2D(z * 3.1, x * 1.3) > 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
315	      for (let y = 1; y <= height; y += 1) world.set(x, topY + y, z, BlockId.Cactus);
316	    }
317	  }
318	}
319	
320	function placeStructures(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
321	  for (let i = 0; i < GEN.houseCount; i += 1) {
322	    const cx = 12 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 24));
323	    const cz = 12 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 24));
324	    placeHouse(world, cx, cz);
325	  }
326	}
327	
328	function placeHouse(world: VoxelWorld, cx: number, cz: number): void {
329	  const half = 3;
330	  const floorY = world.highestSolidY(cx, cz) + 1;
331	  if (floorY < 3 || floorY + 6 >= world.sizeY - 1) return;
332	
333	  for (let x = cx - half - 1; x <= cx + half + 1; x += 1) {
334	    for (let z = cz - half - 1; z <= cz + half + 1; z += 1) {
335	      if (!world.inBounds(x, floorY - 1, z)) return;
336	      const y = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
337	      if (Math.abs(y - (floorY - 1)) > 1) return;
338	    }
339	  }
340	
341	  for (let x = cx - half; x <= cx + half; x += 1) {
342	    for (let z = cz - half; z <= cz + half; z += 1) world.set(x, floorY - 1, z, BlockId.Cobblestone);
343	  }
344	
345	  for (let y = floorY; y <= floorY + 3; y += 1) {
346	    for (let x = cx - half; x <= cx + half; x += 1) {
347	      for (let z = cz - half; z <= cz + half; z += 1) {
348	        const wall = x === cx - half || x === cx + half || z === cz - half || z === cz + half;
349	        if (!wall) continue;
350	        const isWindow = y === floorY + 1 && ((x === cx && (z === cz - half || z === cz + half)) || (z === cz && (x === cx - half || x === cx + half)));
351	        world.set(x, y, z, isWindow ? BlockId.Glass : BlockId.Brick);
352	      }
353	    }
354	  }
355	
356	  world.set(cx, floorY + 1, cz - half, BlockId.Air);
357	  world.set(cx, floorY + 2, cz - half, BlockId.Air);
358	
359	  for (let y = floorY; y <= floorY + 3; y += 1) {
360	    world.set(cx - half, y, cz - half, BlockId.Wood);
361	    world.set(cx + half, y, cz - half, BlockId.Wood);
362	    world.set(cx - half, y, cz + half, BlockId.Wood);
363	    world.set(cx + half, y, cz + half, BlockId.Wood);
364	  }
365	
366	  for (let x = cx - half - 1; x <= cx + half + 1; x += 1) {
367	    for (let z = cz - half - 1; z <= cz + half + 1; z += 1) world.set(x, floorY + 4, z, BlockId.Planks);
368	  }
369	}
370
```

```
161	/** Immutable view for the React UI, replaced only when a visible value changes. */
162	export type GameSnapshot = {
163	  /** Stable handle for dispatching intents; null until the engine exists. */
164	  api: GameApi | null;
165	  inventory: InventorySlot[];
166	  equippedArmor: EquippedArmor;
167	  selectedSlot: number;
168	  hearts: number;
169	  hunger: number;
170	  daylightPercent: number;
171	  passiveCount: number;
172	  hostileCount: number;
173	  respawnSeconds: number;
174	  inventoryOpen: boolean;
175	  paused: boolean;
176	  debugOpen: boolean;
177	  debug: DebugInfo | null;
178	  cameraMode: CameraMode;
179	  /** Total defense points of equipped armor — drives the HUD armor bar. */
180	  armorPoints: number;
181	  capsActive: boolean;
182	  /** True during the sleep fade — drives the fade-to-black overlay. */
183	  sleeping: boolean;
184	  /** Open crafting station (gates smelting recipes in the inventory panel). */
185	  craftingStation: "furnace" | null;
186	  /** Contents of the open chest, or null when no chest is open. */
187	  container: InventorySlot[] | null;
188	};
189	
190	/** One-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio, ...). */
191	export type GameEvent =
192	  | { type: "died" }
193	  | { type: "respawned" }
194	  | { type: "blockBroken"; blockId: BlockId; x: number; y: number; z: number }
195	  | { type: "blockPlaced"; blockId: BlockId; x: number; y: number; z: number }
196	  | { type: "playerHurt" }
197	  | { type: "ateFood" }
198	  | { type: "jumped" }
199	  | { type: "landed"; impact: number }
200	  | { type: "mobAttacked"; kind: MobKind }
201	  | { type: "mobHit"; kind: MobKind }
202	  | { type: "mobDied"; kind: MobKind; x: number; y: number; z: number }
203	  | { type: "attackSwung" }
204	  | { type: "sleepStarted" }
205	  | { type: "sleepDenied"; reason: "daylight" | "hostiles" }
206	  | { type: "wokeUp" }
207	  | { type: "tilledSoil" }
208	  | { type: "plantedSeed" }
209	  | { type: "openedStation"; station: "furnace" }
210	  | { type: "openedContainer" }
211	  | { type: "breakBlocked"; reason: "containerFull" }
212	  | { type: "smelted" }
213	  | { type: "mobFed"; kind: MobKind }
214	  | { type: "mobBred"; kind: MobKind }
215	  | { type: "pickedUp"; items: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> };
216	
217	export type EmitGameEvent = (event: GameEvent) => void;
218
```

```
48	export type GameEngineOptions = {
49	  /** A parsed save to restore, or null for a fresh world. */
50	  save?: SaveData | null;
51	  /** Seed for a fresh world; ignored when a save is provided. */
52	  seed?: number;
53	  /** Randomness source for mob spawning/AI — injectable for deterministic tests. */
54	  rng?: () => number;
55	  /** World dimensions override for fast headless tests. */
56	  worldSize?: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
57	};
58	
59	/**
60	 * The framework-agnostic game core. Owns all simulation state, advances it in
61	 * step(), and accepts player intents through dispatch(). No React, no DOM, no
62	 * rendering — the renderer reads state, the React shell subscribes to
63	 * snapshots via subscribe()/getSnapshot() (useSyncExternalStore compatible).
64	 */
65	export class GameEngine {
66	  readonly state: GameState;
67	  private readonly rng: () => number;
68	  private readonly surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
69	  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
70	  private events: GameEvent[] = [];
71	  private snapshot: GameSnapshot;
72	
73	  constructor(options: GameEngineOptions = {}) {
74	    const save = options.save ?? null;
75	    this.rng = options.rng ?? Math.random;
76	
77	    const seed = save?.seed ?? options.seed ?? Math.floor(Math.random() * 2147483647);
78	    const size = options.worldSize ?? { x: WORLD_SIZE_X, y: WORLD_SIZE_Y, z: WORLD_SIZE_Z };
79	    const world = new VoxelWorld(size.x, size.y, size.z, seed);
80	    generateWorld(world);
81	
82	    const blockChanges = createBlockChangeTracker(world);
83	    if (save) blockChanges.applySavedChanges(save.changes);
84	
85	    this.surfaceYAt = createSurfaceYAt(world);
86	
87	    const firstSpawn = findSpawnOnLand(world, Math.floor(world.sizeX / 2), Math.floor(world.sizeZ / 2));
88	    this.state = {
89	      world,
90	      blockChanges,
91	      player: {
92	        position: new THREE.Vector3(firstSpawn.x, firstSpawn.y, firstSpawn.z),
93	        velocity: new THREE.Vector3(),
94	        yaw: 0,
95	        pitch: 0,
96	        onGround: false
97	      },
98	      inventory: createInitialInventory(),
99	      equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
100	      selectedSlot: 0,
101	      hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
102	      hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
103	      isDead: false,
104	      respawnTimer: 0,
105	      inventoryOpen: false,
106	      craftingStation: null,
107	      containers: new Map(),
108	      openContainerIndex: null,
109	      paused: false,
110	      debugOpen: false,
111	      debugInfo: null,
112	      cameraMode: "first",
113	      capsActive: false,
114	      mobs: [],
115	      nextMobId: 1,
116	      dayClock: 0,
117	      daylight: daylightAt(0),
118	      daylightPercent: Math.round(daylightAt(0) * 100),
119	      weather: { kind: "clear", intensity: 0 },
120	      sleepTimer: 0,
121	      spawnPoint: null,
122	      mining: { targetKey: "", progress: 0 },
123	      timers: createTimers(),
124	      worldMeshDirty: true
125	    };
126	
127	    if (save) {
128	      this.state.inventory = restoreInventorySlots(save) ?? this.state.inventory;
129	      this.state.equippedArmor = restoreEquippedArmor(save) ?? this.state.equippedArmor;
130	      this.state.selectedSlot = restoreSelectedSlot(save) ?? this.state.selectedSlot;
131	      this.state.hearts = restoreHearts(save) ?? this.state.hearts;
132	      this.state.hunger = restoreHungerLevel(save) ?? this.state.hunger;
133	      this.state.spawnPoint = restoreSpawnPoint(save);
134	      // Restore chest contents only for indices that still hold a Chest block.
135	      for (const { index, slots } of readContainers(save)) {
136	        if (index >= 0 && index < world.blocks.length && world.blocks[index] === BlockId.Chest) {
137	          this.state.containers.set(index, slots);
138	        }
139	      }
140	      const savedClock = restoreDayClock(save);
141	      if (savedClock !== null) {
142	        this.state.dayClock = savedClock;
143	        this.state.daylight = daylightAt(savedClock);
144	        this.state.daylightPercent = Math.round(this.state.daylight * 100);
145	      }
146	      if (save.player) this.state.player.position.set(save.player.x, save.player.y, save.player.z);
147	    }
148	
149	    // Safety check: if stuck after load, relocate to a plain.
150	    if (collidesAt(world, this.state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || this.state.player.position.y < 2) {
151	      this.forceUnstuck();
152	    }
153	
154	    spawnInitialMobs(this.state, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
155	    // Seed weather from the (possibly restored) dayClock + player position so a
156	    // loaded save's first frame/snapshot is consistent before the first step().
157	    tickWeather(this.state);
158	    this.snapshot = this.buildSnapshot();
159	  }
160	
161	  /** Advances the simulation by dt seconds. The renderer draws the state afterwards. */
162	  step(dt: number, input: FrameInput): void {
163	    const state = this.state;
164	    if (state.paused) {
165	      // Full freeze: mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all stop.
166	      this.refreshSnapshot();
167	      return;
168	    }
169	    state.capsActive = input.capsActive;
170	
171	    // Stuck detection / auto-unstuck.
172	    const inBadState = collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2;
173	    state.timers.stuckTimer = inBadState ? state.timers.stuckTimer + dt : 0;
174	    if (state.timers.stuckTimer > STUCK_RESET_SECONDS) {
175	      this.forceUnstuck();
176	      state.timers.stuckTimer = 0;
177	    }
178	
179	    // Death: only mobs and the respawn countdown tick while dead.
180	    if (state.isDead) {
181	      if (tickRespawnTimer(state, dt)) this.respawn();
182	      else tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
183	      this.refreshSnapshot();
184	      return;
185	    }
186	
187	    // Sleeping: a full freeze during the fade, then a jump to the next morning.
188	    if (state.sleepTimer > 0) {
189	      state.sleepTimer = Math.max(0, state.sleepTimer - dt);
190	      if (state.sleepTimer === 0) this.wakeToMorning();
191	      this.refreshSnapshot();
192	      return;
193	    }
194	
195	    const move = tickPlayerMotion(state, input, dt, this.applyDamage);
196	    if (move.didJump) this.emit({ type: "jumped" });
197	    if (move.didLand) this.emit({ type: "landed", impact: move.landImpact });
198	    tickHungerDrain(state, move);
199	    tickHealthRegen(state, dt);
200	    tickMining(state, input, dt, this.emit, this.rng);
201	    tickDayNight(state, dt);
202	    tickWeather(state);
203	    tickRandomBlocks(state, dt, this.rng);
204	    tickHostileSpawnDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
205	    tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
206	    tickBreeding(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt, this.emit);
207	    this.tickDebugInfo(dt);
208	
209	    this.refreshSnapshot();
210	  }
211	
212	  /** Applies a discrete player intent. */
213	  dispatch(command: Command): void {
214	    const state = this.state;
215	    switch (command.type) {
216	      case "selectSlot": {
217	        if (command.index >= 0 && command.index < Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS, state.inventory.length)) {
218	          state.selectedSlot = command.index;
219	        }
220	        break;
221	      }
222	      case "toggleInventory": {
223	        state.inventoryOpen = !state.inventoryOpen;
224	        if (!state.inventoryOpen) {
225	          state.craftingStation = null; // leaving the panel closes the station
226	          state.openContainerIndex = null; // ...and the open chest
227	        }
228	        break;
229	      }
230	      case "craft": {
231	        const recipe = RECIPES.find((entry) => entry.id === command.recipeId);
232	        if (!recipe || state.isDead) break;
233	        // Station recipes (e.g. furnace smelting) require that station to be open.
234	        // The UI gates these too, but dispatch is the spoofable surface to guard.
235	        if (recipe.station && recipe.station !== state.craftingStation) break;
236	        const next = inv.craft(state.inventory, recipe);
237	        if (!next) break;
238	        state.inventory = next;
239	        if (recipe.station) this.emit({ type: "smelted" });
240	        break;
241	      }
242	      case "swapSlots": {
243	        state.inventory = inv.swapSlots(state.inventory, command.from, command.to) ?? state.inventory;
244	        break;
245	      }
246	      case "moveStack": {
247	        this.applyMoveStack(command.from, command.to);
248	        break;
249	      }
250	      case "toggleEquipArmor": {
251	        state.equippedArmor = inv.toggleEquipArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor, command.index) ?? state.equippedArmor;
252	        break;
253	      }
254	      case "eatFood": {
255	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
256	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
257	        if (!slot?.id || slot.kind !== "food" || !slot.hunger || slot.count <= 0) break;
258	        const next = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
259	        if (!next) break;
260	        state.inventory = next;
261	        state.hunger = restoreHunger(state.hunger, slot.hunger);
262	        this.emit({ type: "ateFood" });
263	        break;
264	      }
265	      case "placeBlock": {
266	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
267	        // Right-click precedence: feed an aimed animal, then interact with the
268	        // aimed block (bed, furnace), then use the held item (hoe, seeds); only
269	        // place a block if none of those consumed the click.
270	        if (tryFeedAimedMob(state, this.emit)) break;
271	        if (tryInteractBlock(state, this.emit)) break;
272	        if (tryUseHeldItem(state, this.emit, this.rng)) break;
273	        placeSelectedBlock(state, this.emit);
274	        break;
275	      }
276	      case "attack": {
277	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
278	        this.emit({ type: "attackSwung" });
279	        const hitKind = tryAttackMob(state, weaponDamage(state), this.removeMobAt);
280	        if (hitKind) {
281	          this.emit({ type: "mobHit", kind: hitKind });
282	          state.inventory = inv.consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
283	          resetMining(state);
284	        }
285	        break;
286	      }
287	      case "unstuck": {
288	        if (state.isDead) break;
289	        this.forceUnstuck();
290	        break;
291	      }
292	      case "pause": {
293	        // The inventory panel and the death screen own their lock-loss; only
294	        // plain gameplay lock-loss (or an explicit Escape) opens the pause menu.
295	        // Sleeping is a brief, atomic freeze — pausing mid-fade would stall the
296	        // sleep timer (step early-returns on paused before the sleep branch).
297	        if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
298	        state.paused = true;
299	        state.craftingStation = null;
300	        state.openContainerIndex = null;
301	        break;
302	      }
303	      case "resume": {
304	        state.paused = false;
305	        break;
306	      }
307	      case "toggleDebug": {
308	        state.debugOpen = !state.debugOpen;
309	        state.debugInfo = state.debugOpen ? this.currentDebugInfo() : null;
310	        break;
311	      }
312	      case "toggleCameraView": {
313	        // Render-only, so it works even while dead or paused (like Minecraft F5).
314	        state.cameraMode = nextCameraMode(state.cameraMode);
315	        break;
316	      }
317	      case "respawn": {
318	        // Skip the rest of the countdown; the next step performs the respawn.
319	        if (state.isDead) state.respawnTimer = 0;
320	        break;
321	      }
322	    }
323	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
324	    this.refreshSnapshot();
325	  }
326	
327	  /** Mouse-look: applied directly by the input controller (radians). */
328	  applyLook(deltaYaw: number, deltaPitch: number): void {
329	    const player = this.state.player;
330	    player.yaw += deltaYaw;
331	    player.pitch = Math.max(-Math.PI / 2 + 0.01, Math.min(Math.PI / 2 - 0.01, player.pitch + deltaPitch));
332	  }
333	
334	  /** Current world + player state as a persistable save. */
335	  serialize(): SaveData {
336	    const state = this.state;
337	    return {
338	      version: 4,
339	      seed: state.world.seed,
340	      changes: state.blockChanges.changes(),
341	      inventorySlots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(state.inventory),
342	      equippedArmor: { ...state.equippedArmor },
343	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
344	      player: {
345	        x: state.player.position.x,
346	        y: state.player.position.y,
347	        z: state.player.position.z
348	      },
349	      dayClock: state.dayClock,
350	      hearts: state.hearts,
351	      hunger: state.hunger,
352	      spawnPoint: state.spawnPoint ? { ...state.spawnPoint } : null,
353	      blockEntities: serializeContainers(state.containers)
354	    };
355	  }
356	
357	  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
358	    this.listeners.add(listener);
359	    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
360	  };
361	
362	  getSnapshot = (): GameSnapshot => this.snapshot;
363	
364	  /** Drains queued one-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio). */
365	  consumeEvents(): GameEvent[] {
366	    if (this.events.length === 0) return this.events;
367	    const drained = this.events;
368	    this.events = [];
369	    return drained;
370	  }
371	
372	  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {
373	    this.events.push(event);
374	  };
375	
376	  /**
377	   * Moves a slot between the player inventory and the open chest. Indices at or
378	   * above CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE address the container; below it, the inventory.
379	   * Writes back whichever of the two arrays the move touched.
380	   */
381	  private applyMoveStack(from: number, to: number): void {
382	    const state = this.state;
383	    const container = state.openContainerIndex !== null ? state.containers.get(state.openContainerIndex) : undefined;
384	    const resolve = (index: number): { arr: InventorySlot[]; local: number } | null => {
385	      if (index >= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE) return container ? { arr: container, local: index - CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE } : null;
386	      return { arr: state.inventory, local: index };
387	    };
388	    const a = resolve(from);
389	    const b = resolve(to);
390	    if (!a || !b) return;
391	    const moved = inv.moveStack(a.arr, a.local, b.arr, b.local);
392	    if (!moved) return;
393	
394	    // Resolve each side's destination BEFORE writing: the first write reassigns
395	    // state.inventory, which would make a later `arr === state.inventory` check
396	    // stale and misroute an inventory↔inventory move into the open chest.
397	    const aIsInventory = a.arr === state.inventory;
398	    const bIsInventory = b.arr === state.inventory;
399	    const writeBack = (isInventory: boolean, next: InventorySlot[]): void => {
400	      if (isInventory) state.inventory = next;
401	      else if (state.openContainerIndex !== null) state.containers.set(state.openContainerIndex, next);
402	    };
403	    writeBack(aIsInventory, moved.a);
404	    writeBack(bIsInventory, moved.b);
405	  }
406	
407	  private applyDamage = (amount: number): void => {
408	    const heartsBefore = this.state.hearts;
409	    const died = applyDamageWithArmor(this.state, amount);
410	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
411	    if (died) {
412	      resetMining(this.state);
413	      this.emit({ type: "died" });
414	    } else if (this.state.hearts < heartsBefore) {
415	      this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
416	    }
417	  };
418	
419	  private removeMobAt = (index: number): void => {
420	    const state = this.state;
421	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
422	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
423	    // Babies drop nothing — only grown animals yield loot.
424	    const drops = mob.ageTimer <= 0 ? rollMobDrops(mob.kind, this.rng) : [];
425	    for (const drop of drops) {
426	      state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
427	    }
428	    this.emit({ type: "mobDied", kind: mob.kind, x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
429	    // Drops land straight in inventory (no ground item), so announce them.
430	    if (drops.length > 0) this.emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: drops });
431	  };
432	
433	  private get mobTickDeps() {
434	    return {
435	      surfaceYAt: this.surfaceYAt,
436	      applyDamage: this.applyDamage,
437	      removeMobAt: this.removeMobAt,
438	      rng: this.rng,
439	      emit: this.emit
440	    };
441	  }
442	
443	  private forceUnstuck(): void {
444	    const state = this.state;
445	    const safe = findSpawnOnLand(state.world, state.player.position.x, state.player.position.z, true);
446	    state.player.position.set(safe.x, safe.y, safe.z);
447	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
448	    state.player.onGround = false;
449	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
450	  }
451	
452	  private respawn(): void {
453	    const state = this.state;
454	    const bed = state.spawnPoint;
455	    if (bed && state.world.get(bed.x, bed.y, bed.z) === BlockId.Bed) {
456	      // Respawn standing on the bed; the bed block is non-solid head room above it.
457	      state.player.position.set(bed.x + 0.5, bed.y + 1.05, bed.z + 0.5);
458	    } else {
459	      const spawn = randomLandPointNear(state.world, this.surfaceYAt, state.world.sizeX / 2, state.world.sizeZ / 2, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.9, this.rng);
460	      state.player.position.set(spawn.x, spawn.y + 2, spawn.z);
461	    }
462	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
463	    state.player.pitch = 0;
464	    resetMining(state);
465	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
466	    this.events.push({ type: "respawned" });
467	  }
468	
469	  /** Advances the day clock to the next morning after a sleep completes. */
470	  private wakeToMorning(): void {
471	    const state = this.state;
472	    const nextDay = Math.floor(state.dayClock / DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS) + 1;
473	    state.dayClock = (nextDay + WAKE_DAY_PHASE) * DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS;
474	    state.daylight = daylightAt(state.dayClock);
475	    state.daylightPercent = Math.round(state.daylight * 100);
476	    this.emit({ type: "wokeUp" });
477	  }
478	
479	  /** Unequips armor that left the inventory (broken or dropped). */
480	  private syncEquippedArmor(): void {
481	    const state = this.state;
482	    state.equippedArmor = inv.unequipMissingArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor) ?? state.equippedArmor;
483	  }
484	
485	  /** Refreshes the F3 readout at ~4 Hz so React is not re-rendered every frame. */
486	  private tickDebugInfo(dt: number): void {
487	    const state = this.state;
488	    if (!state.debugOpen) return;
489	    state.timers.debugHudTimer += dt;
490	    if (state.timers.debugHudTimer < 0.25) return;
491	    state.timers.debugHudTimer = 0;
492	    state.debugInfo = this.currentDebugInfo();
493	  }
494	
495	  private currentDebugInfo() {
496	    const { player, daylight } = this.state;
497	    return {
498	      x: Math.round(player.position.x * 10) / 10,
499	      y: Math.round(player.position.y * 10) / 10,
500	      z: Math.round(player.position.z * 10) / 10,
501	      daylight: Math.round(daylight * 100) / 100
502	    };
503	  }
504	
505	  private buildSnapshot(): GameSnapshot {
506	    const state = this.state;
507	    return {
508	      api: this,
509	      inventory: state.inventory,
510	      equippedArmor: state.equippedArmor,
511	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
512	      hearts: state.hearts,
513	      hunger: state.hunger,
514	      daylightPercent: state.daylightPercent,
515	      passiveCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 0 : 1), 0),
516	      hostileCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0),
517	      respawnSeconds: state.isDead ? Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.respawnTimer)) : 0,
518	      inventoryOpen: state.inventoryOpen,
519	      paused: state.paused,
520	      debugOpen: state.debugOpen,
521	      debug: state.debugInfo,
522	      cameraMode: state.cameraMode,
523	      armorPoints: inv.equippedDefense(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor),
524	      capsActive: state.capsActive,
525	      sleeping: state.sleepTimer > 0,
526	      craftingStation: state.craftingStation,
527	      container: state.openContainerIndex !== null ? (state.containers.get(state.openContainerIndex) ?? null) : null
528	    };
529	  }
530	
531	  private refreshSnapshot(): void {
532	    const next = this.buildSnapshot();
533	    const prev = this.snapshot;
534	    const changed = (Object.keys(next) as Array<keyof GameSnapshot>).some((key) => next[key] !== prev[key]);
535	    if (!changed) return;
536	    this.snapshot = next;
537	    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
538	  }
539	}
540	
```

```
1	import { CHEST_SLOTS, HOTBAR_SLOTS, INVENTORY_SLOTS, MAX_HEARTS, MAX_HUNGER, MAX_STACK_SIZE } from "@/lib/game/config";
2	import { ARMOR_SLOTS, createEmptyArmorEquipment, createEmptySlot, createSlot, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
3	import type { EquippedArmor, SaveData, SaveDataV1, SaveDataV2, SaveDataV3, SavedContainer, SavedSlot, InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
4	
5	/**
6	 * Migrates a v1 save (40 slots, 10-slot hotbar) to v2 (36 slots, 9-slot
7	 * hotbar): non-empty slots are packed in order, stackable items merge into
8	 * earlier stacks, and anything that still overflows 36 slots is dropped.
9	 */
10	/** Coerces a persisted hotbar index to a finite integer within 0..HOTBAR_SLOTS-1. */
11	function normalizeSelectedSlot(value: number): number {
12	  const index = Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.floor(value) : 0;
13	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS - 1, index));
14	}
15	
16	export function migrateSaveV1toV2(save: SaveDataV1): SaveDataV2 {
17	  const migrated: SaveDataV2 = { ...save, version: 2, selectedSlot: normalizeSelectedSlot(save.selectedSlot) };
18	
19	  if (Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {
20	    const packed: Array<{ id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number }> = [];
21	    for (const saved of save.inventorySlots) {
22	      if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0) continue;
23	      // Items without durability stack; merge them into an earlier stack first.
24	      const stackable = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id] ? !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id].maxDurability : false;
25	      let remaining = saved.count;
26	      if (stackable) {
27	        for (const slot of packed) {
28	          if (slot.id !== saved.id || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) continue;
29	          const moved = Math.min(MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count, remaining);
30	          slot.count += moved;
31	          remaining -= moved;
32	          if (remaining === 0) break;
33	        }
34	      }
35	      if (remaining > 0 && packed.length < INVENTORY_SLOTS) {
36	        packed.push({ id: saved.id, count: remaining, durability: saved.durability });
37	      }
38	    }
39	    migrated.inventorySlots = packed;
40	  }
41	
42	  return migrated;
43	}
44	
45	/**
46	 * Migrates a v2 save to v3 — a pure version bump. The new persisted fields
47	 * (dayClock, hearts, hunger, spawnPoint) are optional, so an older save simply
48	 * loads with the engine's defaults for them.
49	 */
50	export function migrateSaveV2toV3(save: SaveDataV2): SaveDataV3 {
51	  return { ...save, version: 3 };
52	}
53	
54	/**
55	 * Migrates a v3 save to v4 — a pure version bump. `blockEntities` (chest
56	 * contents) is optional, so a pre-chest save simply loads with no containers.
57	 */
58	export function migrateSaveV3toV4(save: SaveDataV3): SaveData {
59	  return { ...save, version: 4 };
60	}
61	
62	// Storage is injectable so save logic can be tested without a browser.
63	export function readSave(saveKey: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): SaveData | null {
64	  try {
65	    const raw = storage.getItem(saveKey);
66	    if (!raw) return null;
67	    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as SaveData | SaveDataV3 | SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV1;
68	    if (!parsed || !Number.isFinite(parsed.seed) || !Array.isArray(parsed.changes)) return null;
69	    let migrated: SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV3 | SaveData = parsed.version === 1 ? migrateSaveV1toV2(parsed) : parsed;
70	    if (migrated.version === 2) migrated = migrateSaveV2toV3(migrated);
71	    if (migrated.version === 3) migrated = migrateSaveV3toV4(migrated);
72	    if (migrated.version !== 4) return null;
73	    return migrated;
74	  } catch {
75	    return null;
76	  }
77	}
78	
79	export function writeSave(saveKey: string, data: SaveData, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {
80	  storage.setItem(saveKey, JSON.stringify(data));
81	}
82	
83	export function inventorySlotsSnapshot(inventory: InventorySlot[]): SavedSlot[] {
84	  return inventory.map((slot) => ({ id: slot.id, count: slot.count, durability: slot.durability }));
85	}
86	
87	/**
88	 * Rebuilds one slot from its saved snapshot: drops unknown ids, clamps the
89	 * count, and clamps/defaults durability for gear (a non-positive saved
90	 * durability means the piece broke and is dropped). Returns an empty slot for
91	 * anything invalid. Shared by inventory and container restore.
92	 */
93	function restoreSlot(saved: SavedSlot | undefined): InventorySlot {
94	  if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0 || !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id]) return createEmptySlot();
95	  const slot = createSlot(saved.id, Math.min(MAX_STACK_SIZE, Math.max(0, Math.floor(saved.count))));
96	  if ((slot.kind === "tool" || slot.kind === "weapon" || slot.kind === "armor") && slot.maxDurability) {
97	    if (typeof saved.durability === "number") {
98	      const loaded = Math.floor(saved.durability);
99	      if (loaded <= 0) return createEmptySlot();
100	      slot.durability = Math.max(1, Math.min(slot.maxDurability, loaded));
101	    } else {
102	      slot.durability = slot.maxDurability;
103	    }
104	  }
105	  return slot;
106	}
107	
108	/** Snapshots non-empty chest containers for persistence (empty chests carry no data). */
109	export function serializeContainers(containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>): SavedContainer[] {
110	  const out: SavedContainer[] = [];
111	  for (const [index, slots] of containers) {
112	    if (!slots.some((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0)) continue;
113	    out.push({ index, slots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(slots) });
114	  }
115	  return out;
116	}
117	
118	/**
119	 * Reads chest containers from a save into validated, CHEST_SLOTS-length slot
120	 * arrays keyed by voxel index. Per-slot validation only — the engine still
121	 * confirms each index actually holds a Chest block before using it.
122	 */
123	export function readContainers(save: SaveData): Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> {
124	  if (!Array.isArray(save.blockEntities)) return [];
125	  const out: Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> = [];
126	  for (const entry of save.blockEntities) {
127	    if (!entry || !Number.isFinite(entry.index) || !Array.isArray(entry.slots)) continue;
128	    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, (_, i) => restoreSlot(entry.slots[i]));
129	    out.push({ index: entry.index, slots });
130	  }
131	  return out;
132	}
133	
134	/**
135	 * Rebuilds inventory slots from a save, dropping unknown items, clamping
136	 * counts and durability, and skipping broken gear. Supports both the current
137	 * inventorySlots shape and the legacy inventoryCounts shape. Returns null when
138	 * the save carries no inventory.
139	 */
140	export function restoreInventorySlots(save: SaveData): InventorySlot[] | null {
141	  if (Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {
142	    const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
143	    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(INVENTORY_SLOTS, save.inventorySlots.length); i += 1) {
144	      slots[i] = restoreSlot(save.inventorySlots[i]);
145	    }
146	    return slots;
147	  }
148	
149	  if (save.inventoryCounts) {
150	    const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
151	    let cursor = 0;
152	    for (const [id, raw] of Object.entries(save.inventoryCounts)) {
153	      if (!ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[id]) continue;
154	      let remaining = Math.max(0, Math.floor(raw));
155	      while (remaining > 0 && cursor < slots.length) {
156	        const add = Math.min(MAX_STACK_SIZE, remaining);
157	        slots[cursor] = createSlot(id, add);
158	        cursor += 1;
159	        remaining -= add;
160	      }
161	    }
162	    return slots;
163	  }
164	
165	  return null;
166	}
167	
168	/** Restores equipped armor from a save, ignoring ids that are not valid armor for the slot. */
169	export function restoreEquippedArmor(save: SaveData): EquippedArmor | null {
170	  if (!save.equippedArmor) return null;
171	  const next = createEmptyArmorEquipment();
172	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
173	    const equippedId = save.equippedArmor[armorSlot];
174	    if (!equippedId) continue;
175	    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[equippedId];
176	    if (def?.kind !== "armor" || def.armorSlot !== armorSlot) continue;
177	    next[armorSlot] = equippedId;
178	  }
179	  return next;
180	}
181	
182	export function restoreSelectedSlot(save: SaveData): number | null {
183	  if (typeof save.selectedSlot !== "number") return null;
184	  return normalizeSelectedSlot(save.selectedSlot);
185	}
186	
187	/** Restores the day clock from a save (finite, non-negative); null if absent/invalid. */
188	export function restoreDayClock(save: SaveData): number | null {
189	  if (typeof save.dayClock !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.dayClock) || save.dayClock < 0) return null;
190	  return save.dayClock;
191	}
192	
193	/** Restores hearts clamped to 1..MAX_HEARTS; null if absent/invalid. */
194	export function restoreHearts(save: SaveData): number | null {
195	  if (typeof save.hearts !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.hearts)) return null;
196	  return Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_HEARTS, Math.floor(save.hearts)));
197	}
198	
199	/** Restores hunger clamped to 0..MAX_HUNGER; null if absent/invalid. */
200	export function restoreHungerLevel(save: SaveData): number | null {
201	  if (typeof save.hunger !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.hunger)) return null;
202	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_HUNGER, Math.floor(save.hunger)));
203	}
204	
205	/** Restores the bed respawn point; null if absent or explicitly cleared. */
206	export function restoreSpawnPoint(save: SaveData): { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null {
207	  const sp = save.spawnPoint;
208	  if (!sp || !Number.isFinite(sp.x) || !Number.isFinite(sp.y) || !Number.isFinite(sp.z)) return null;
209	  return { x: Math.floor(sp.x), y: Math.floor(sp.y), z: Math.floor(sp.z) };
210	}
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId } from "@/lib/world";
3	
4	export type ItemKind = "block" | "weapon" | "tool" | "armor" | "food" | "material";
5	export type ArmorSlot = "helmet" | "face_mask" | "neck_protection" | "chestplate" | "leggings" | "boots";
6	export type EquippedArmor = Record<ArmorSlot, string | null>;
7	
8	export type ItemDef = {
9	  id: string;
10	  label: string;
11	  kind: ItemKind;
12	  blockId?: BlockId;
13	  attack?: number;
14	  minePower?: number;
15	  mineTier?: number;
16	  armorSlot?: ArmorSlot;
17	  defense?: number;
18	  maxDurability?: number;
19	  /** Hunger points restored when eaten (food items only). */
20	  hunger?: number;
21	};
22	
23	export type InventorySlot = {
24	  id: string | null;
25	  label: string;
26	  kind: ItemKind | null;
27	  count: number;
28	  blockId?: BlockId;
29	  attack?: number;
30	  minePower?: number;
31	  mineTier?: number;
32	  armorSlot?: ArmorSlot;
33	  defense?: number;
34	  durability?: number;
35	  maxDurability?: number;
36	  hunger?: number;
37	};
38	
39	export type Recipe = {
40	  id: string;
41	  label: string;
42	  cost: Array<{ slotId: string; count: number }>;
43	  result: { slotId: string; count: number };
44	  /** Crafting station required; omitted means the basic crafting grid. */
45	  station?: "furnace";
46	};
47	
48	export type MobKind = "sheep" | "chicken" | "horse" | "zombie" | "skeleton" | "spider";
49	
50	export type MobModel = {
51	  group: THREE.Group;
52	  legs: THREE.Mesh[];
53	  halfHeight: number;
54	  materials: THREE.Material[];
55	  geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[];
56	};
57	
58	/** A persisted inventory/container slot: just enough to rebuild it on load. */
59	export type SavedSlot = { id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number };
60	
61	/** A block-entity: a placed chest's contents, keyed by its voxel index. */
62	export type SavedContainer = { index: number; slots: SavedSlot[] };
63	
64	/** Legacy save shape (40 inventory slots, 10-slot hotbar) — accepted and migrated on load. */
65	export type SaveDataV1 = {
66	  version: 1;
67	  seed: number;
68	  changes: Array<[number, number]>;
69	  inventoryCounts?: Record<string, number>;
70	  inventorySlots?: SavedSlot[];
71	  equippedArmor?: Partial<EquippedArmor>;
72	  selectedSlot: number;
73	  player: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
74	};
75	
76	/** v2 save shape: same fields as v1, reinterpreted for 36 slots / 9-slot hotbar. */
77	export type SaveDataV2 = Omit<SaveDataV1, "version"> & { version: 2 };
78	
79	/**
80	 * v3 save shape: v2 plus persisted time-of-day, player stats, and the bed
81	 * respawn point. All added fields are optional so the v2→v3 migration is a pure
82	 * version bump and pre-v3 saves load with sensible defaults.
83	 */
84	export type SaveDataV3 = Omit<SaveDataV2, "version"> & {
85	  version: 3;
86	  dayClock?: number;
87	  hearts?: number;
88	  hunger?: number;
89	  spawnPoint?: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null;
90	};
91	
92	/**
93	 * Current save shape (v4): v3 plus chest contents as block-entities. The new
94	 * field is optional so the v3→v4 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v4
95	 * saves load with no containers. `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged — chests are never
96	 * generated, so worldgen and the block-diff index space are untouched.
97	 */
98	export type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV3, "version"> & {
99	  version: 4;
100	  blockEntities?: SavedContainer[];
101	};
102
```
