1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId, voxelRaycast } from "@/lib/world";
3	import {
4	  BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS,
5	  CHEST_SLOTS,
6	  EYE_HEIGHT,
7	  MINE_REACH,
8	  SLEEP_ALLOWED_BELOW_DAYLIGHT,
9	  SLEEP_FADE_SECONDS,
10	  SLEEP_HOSTILE_RADIUS
11	} from "@/lib/game/config";
12	import { adjustSlotCount, consumeToolDurability } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
13	import { createEmptySlot } from "@/lib/game/items";
14	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
15	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState } from "../state";
16	import { findAimedMobIndex } from "./combat";
17	import { lookDirection } from "./playerMotion";
18	
19	const scratchEye = new THREE.Vector3();
20	const scratchDir = new THREE.Vector3();
21	
22	/** Blocks whose right-click runs a handler instead of placing the held block. */
23	export type InteractiveKind = "bed" | "furnace" | "chest";
24	
25	export const INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS: Partial<Record<BlockId, InteractiveKind>> = {
26	  [BlockId.Bed]: "bed",
27	  [BlockId.Furnace]: "furnace",
28	  [BlockId.Chest]: "chest"
29	};
30	
31	/**
32	 * Right-click "use" on the aimed block. Returns true when the click was an
33	 * interaction (consumed — the caller must NOT then place a block), false when
34	 * the aimed block has no behavior and placement should proceed.
35	 *
36	 * This is the shared hook future interactive blocks (furnace, …) plug into:
37	 * add a `BlockId → kind` entry and a branch below.
38	 */
39	export function tryInteractBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
40	  const { world, player } = state;
41	  scratchEye.set(player.position.x, player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, player.position.z);
42	  lookDirection(player.yaw, player.pitch, scratchDir);
43	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, scratchEye, scratchDir, MINE_REACH);
44	  if (!result) return false;
45	
46	  const block = world.get(result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z) as BlockId;
47	  const kind = INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS[block];
48	  if (!kind) return false;
49	
50	  if (kind === "bed") return interactBed(state, emit, result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z);
51	  if (kind === "furnace") return interactFurnace(state, emit);
52	  if (kind === "chest") return interactChest(state, emit, result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z);
53	  return false;
54	}
55	
56	/** Opens the chest at (x,y,z) in the inventory panel, creating its (lazy) empty store. */
57	function interactChest(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
58	  const idx = state.world.index(x, y, z);
59	  if (!state.containers.has(idx)) {
60	    state.containers.set(
61	      idx,
62	      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())
63	    );
64	  }
65	  state.openContainerIndex = idx;
66	  state.inventoryOpen = true;
67	  emit({ type: "openedContainer" });
68	  return true;
69	}
70	
71	/** What each breedable animal is fed to enter "in love" mode. */
72	const FEED_ITEMS: Partial<Record<MobKind, string>> = {
73	  sheep: "wheat",
74	  horse: "wheat",
75	  chicken: "seeds"
76	};
77	
78	/**
79	 * Right-click an adult animal with its feed item to put it "in love" (toward
80	 * breeding). First in the right-click precedence so feeding wins over placing or
81	 * tilling when an animal is in the crosshair. Returns true when an animal was fed.
82	 */
83	export function tryFeedAimedMob(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
84	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
85	  if (!slot?.id || slot.count <= 0) return false;
86	  const index = findAimedMobIndex(state);
87	  if (index < 0) return false;
88	  const mob = state.mobs[index];
89	  if (mob.hostile || FEED_ITEMS[mob.kind] !== slot.id) return false;
90	  if (mob.ageTimer > 0 || mob.fedTimer > 0) return false; // babies and already-in-love animals decline
91	
92	  state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
93	  mob.fedTimer = BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS;
94	  emit({ type: "mobFed", kind: mob.kind });
95	  return true;
96	}
97	
98	/** Opens the inventory in furnace mode so its smelting recipes unlock. */
99	function interactFurnace(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
100	  state.inventoryOpen = true;
101	  state.craftingStation = "furnace";
102	  emit({ type: "openedStation", station: "furnace" });
103	  return true;
104	}
105	
106	/** Sleep in a bed: only at night, only when no hostile is near. Sets the respawn point. */
107	function interactBed(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
108	  if (state.daylight >= SLEEP_ALLOWED_BELOW_DAYLIGHT) {
109	    emit({ type: "sleepDenied", reason: "daylight" });
110	    return true;
111	  }
112	  for (const mob of state.mobs) {
113	    if (mob.hostile && mob.position.distanceTo(state.player.position) <= SLEEP_HOSTILE_RADIUS) {
114	      emit({ type: "sleepDenied", reason: "hostiles" });
115	      return true;
116	    }
117	  }
118	
119	  state.spawnPoint = { x, y, z };
120	  state.sleepTimer = SLEEP_FADE_SECONDS;
121	  emit({ type: "sleepStarted" });
122	  return true;
123	}
124	
125	/**
126	 * Right-click "use" of the held item on the aimed block: a hoe tills grass/dirt
127	 * into farmland, seeds plant wheat on farmland. Returns true when an action
128	 * happened (consumes the click), false to fall through to block placement.
129	 */
130	export function tryUseHeldItem(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): boolean {
131	  void rng; // reserved for future randomized uses (bone meal, …)
132	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
133	  if (!slot?.id || slot.count <= 0) return false;
134	  const isHoe = slot.id.endsWith("_hoe");
135	  const isSeeds = slot.id === "seeds";
136	  if (!isHoe && !isSeeds) return false;
137	
138	  const { world, player } = state;
139	  scratchEye.set(player.position.x, player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, player.position.z);
140	  lookDirection(player.yaw, player.pitch, scratchDir);
141	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, scratchEye, scratchDir, MINE_REACH);
142	  if (!result) return false;
143	  const { x, y, z } = result.hit;
144	  const block = world.get(x, y, z) as BlockId;
145	
146	  if (isHoe) {
147	    if (block !== BlockId.Grass && block !== BlockId.Dirt) return false;
148	    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Farmland);
149	    state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
150	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
151	    emit({ type: "tilledSoil" });
152	    return true;
153	  }
154	
155	  // Seeds: plant on farmland when the cell above is clear.
156	  if (block !== BlockId.Farmland || world.get(x, y + 1, z) !== BlockId.Air) return false;
157	  state.blockChanges.set(x, y + 1, z, BlockId.WheatStage0);
158	  state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
159	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
160	  emit({ type: "plantedSeed" });
161	  return true;
162	}
163

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { HOSTILE_CAP, HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT, HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS, RENDER_RADIUS } from "@/lib/game/config";
3	import { MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
4	import { randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
5	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
6	import type { GameState, MobState } from "../state";
7	
8	export type SpawnGroupArgs = {
9	  kind: MobKind;
10	  hostile: boolean;
11	  count: number;
12	  centerX: number;
13	  centerZ: number;
14	  radius: number;
15	};
16	
17	export function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
18	  const template = MOB_TEMPLATES[args.kind];
19	  const halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(args.kind);
20	  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {
21	    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng);
22	    const mob: MobState = {
23	      id: state.nextMobId,
24	      kind: args.kind,
25	      hostile: args.hostile,
26	      hp: template.hp,
27	      position: new THREE.Vector3(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y + halfHeight, spawnPos.z),
28	      direction: new THREE.Vector3(rng() - 0.5, 0, rng() - 0.5).normalize(),
29	      yaw: 0,
30	      turnTimer: 1 + rng() * 3,
31	      speed: template.speed,
32	      moveSpeed: template.speed,
33	      detectRange: template.detectRange,
34	      attackDamage: template.attackDamage,
35	      attackCooldown: template.attackCooldown,
36	      attackTimer: rng(),
37	      halfHeight,
38	      bobSeed: rng() * 10,
39	      fedTimer: 0,
40	      ageTimer: 0
41	    };
42	    state.nextMobId += 1;
43	    state.mobs.push(mob);
44	  }
45	}
46	
47	/**
48	 * The day-one population around the player's spawn point. Passives scatter
49	 * over a wider ring than hostiles so the spawn area doesn't feel like a
50	 * petting zoo; hostiles stay closer (the dawn-aggro behavior tests document).
51	 */
52	export function spawnInitialMobs(state: GameState, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
53	  const centerX = state.player.position.x;
54	  const centerZ = state.player.position.z;
55	  const passiveRadius = RENDER_RADIUS * 1.2;
56	  const hostileRadius = RENDER_RADIUS * 0.7;
57	  const groups: Array<[MobKind, boolean, number, number]> = [
58	    ["sheep", false, 6, passiveRadius],
59	    ["chicken", false, 5, passiveRadius],
60	    ["horse", false, 3, passiveRadius],
61	    ["zombie", true, 8, hostileRadius],
62	    ["skeleton", true, 6, hostileRadius],
63	    ["spider", true, 6, hostileRadius]
64	  ];
65	  for (const [kind, hostile, count, radius] of groups) {
66	    spawnMobGroup(state, { kind, hostile, count, centerX, centerZ, radius }, rng, surfaceYAt);
67	  }
68	}
69	
70	/** Trickles hostile mobs in around the player at night, up to the cap. */
71	export function tickHostileSpawnDirector(state: GameState, dt: number, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
72	  state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer += dt;
73	  if (state.daylight >= HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT || state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer < HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS) return;
74	  state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer = 0;
75	
76	  const livingHostiles = state.mobs.filter((mob) => mob.hostile).length;
77	  if (livingHostiles >= HOSTILE_CAP) return;
78	
79	  const spawnKinds: Array<"zombie" | "skeleton" | "spider"> = ["zombie", "skeleton", "spider"];
80	  const kind = spawnKinds[Math.floor(rng() * spawnKinds.length)];
81	  spawnMobGroup(
82	    state,
83	    {
84	      kind,
85	      hostile: true,
86	      count: 1 + (rng() > 0.7 ? 1 : 0),
87	      centerX: state.player.position.x,
88	      centerZ: state.player.position.z,
89	      radius: Math.max(26, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.85)
90	    },
91	    rng,
92	    surfaceYAt
93	  );
94	}
95

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId, collidesAt, voxelRaycast } from "@/lib/world";
3	import { BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER, CHEST_SLOTS, EYE_HEIGHT, MINE_REACH, MINING_RATE, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT } from "@/lib/game/config";
4	import { BREAK_HARDNESS, createEmptySlot, rollBlockDrops } from "@/lib/game/items";
5	import { adjustSlotCount, consumeToolDurability, tryInsertSlots } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
6	import type { EmitGameEvent, FrameInput, GameState } from "../state";
7	import { lookDirection } from "./playerMotion";
8	import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
9	
10	const scratchEye = new THREE.Vector3();
11	const scratchDir = new THREE.Vector3();
12	
13	function eyePosition(state: GameState, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
14	  const { position } = state.player;
15	  return out.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
16	}
17	
18	export function selectedTool(state: GameState): InventorySlot | null {
19	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
20	  return slot?.kind === "tool" && slot.count > 0 ? slot : null;
21	}
22	
23	export function canMineBlock(block: BlockId, toolTier: number): boolean {
24	  if (block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Brick) return toolTier >= 1;
25	  if (block === BlockId.SliverOre) return toolTier >= 2;
26	  if (block === BlockId.RubyOre) return toolTier >= 3;
27	  if (block === BlockId.GoldOre) return toolTier >= 3;
28	  if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre) return toolTier >= 4;
29	  if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre) return toolTier >= 4;
30	  return true;
31	}
32	
33	export function miningSpeed(tool: InventorySlot | null): number {
34	  return tool?.minePower ?? BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER;
35	}
36	
37	export function resetMining(state: GameState): void {
38	  state.mining.targetKey = "";
39	  state.mining.progress = 0;
40	}
41	
42	function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {
43	  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {
44	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
45	  }
46	}
47	
48	/**
49	 * Empties a broken chest into the inventory. Returns true if the break may
50	 * proceed (the chest was empty, or its contents all fit), false to refuse it
51	 * (no room — the chest is left intact). On success the container entry is
52	 * removed and a pickedUp toast announces what was retrieved.
53	 */
54	function spillChestOnBreak(state: GameState, idx: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
55	  const container = state.containers.get(idx);
56	  const items = container?.filter((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0) ?? [];
57	  if (items.length > 0) {
58	    const merged = tryInsertSlots(state.inventory, items);
59	    if (!merged) {
60	      emit({ type: "breakBlocked", reason: "containerFull" });
61	      return false;
62	    }
63	    state.inventory = merged;
64	    const picked = new Map<string, number>();
65	    for (const slot of items) picked.set(slot.id!, (picked.get(slot.id!) ?? 0) + slot.count);
66	    emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: [...picked].map(([itemId, count]) => ({ itemId, count })) });
67	  }
68	  state.containers.delete(idx);
69	  return true;
70	}
71	
72	/** Advances mining progress while the mouse is held; breaks the block at full progress. */
73	export function tickMining(state: GameState, input: FrameInput, dt: number, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): void {
74	  if (!input.leftMouseHeld) {
75	    // Releasing the button abandons progress (matching the crack overlay).
76	    if (state.mining.progress > 0) resetMining(state);
77	    return;
78	  }
79	  if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || !input.pointerLocked) return;
80	
81	  const { world, mining } = state;
82	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
83	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
84	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
85	  if (!result) {
86	    resetMining(state);
87	    return;
88	  }
89	
90	  const bx = result.hit.x;
91	  const by = result.hit.y;
92	  const bz = result.hit.z;
93	  const targetBlock = world.get(bx, by, bz);
94	  const tool = selectedTool(state);
95	  const tier = tool?.mineTier ?? 0;
96	
97	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Bedrock || targetBlock === BlockId.Air || !canMineBlock(targetBlock as BlockId, tier)) {
98	    resetMining(state);
99	    return;
100	  }
101	
102	  const key = `${bx},${by},${bz}`;
103	  if (mining.targetKey !== key) {
104	    mining.targetKey = key;
105	    mining.progress = 0;
106	  }
107	
108	  const hardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[targetBlock as BlockId] ?? 2;
109	  mining.progress += dt * miningSpeed(tool) * MINING_RATE;
110	  if (mining.progress < hardness) return;
111	
112	  // Breaking a chest empties it into the inventory first; if it does not all
113	  // fit, refuse the break so nothing is lost (the chest stays intact).
114	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Chest && !spillChestOnBreak(state, world.index(bx, by, bz), emit)) {
115	    resetMining(state);
116	    return;
117	  }
118	
119	  state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
120	  if (tool) state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
121	  addBlockDrop(state, targetBlock as BlockId, rng);
122	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
123	  resetMining(state);
124	  emit({ type: "blockBroken", blockId: targetBlock as BlockId, x: bx, y: by, z: bz });
125	}
126	
127	/** Places the selected block against the targeted face, refusing self-entombment. */
128	export function placeSelectedBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
129	  const { world } = state;
130	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
131	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
132	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
133	  if (!result) return;
134	
135	  const tx = result.previous.x;
136	  const ty = result.previous.y;
137	  const tz = result.previous.z;
138	  if (!world.inBounds(tx, ty, tz) || world.get(tx, ty, tz) !== BlockId.Air) return;
139	
140	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
141	  if (!slot || !slot.id || slot.kind !== "block" || slot.count <= 0 || slot.blockId === undefined) return;
142	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Bedrock) return;
143	
144	  const afterTake = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
145	  if (!afterTake) return;
146	  state.inventory = afterTake;
147	  state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, slot.blockId);
148	  if (collidesAt(world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
149	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, BlockId.Air);
150	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, 1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
151	    return;
152	  }
153	
154	  // A placed chest gets a fresh, empty block-entity store.
155	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Chest) {
156	    state.containers.set(
157	      world.index(tx, ty, tz),
158	      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())
159	    );
160	  }
161	
162	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
163	  emit({ type: "blockPlaced", blockId: slot.blockId, x: tx, y: ty, z: tz });
164	}
165

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BiomeId, BlockId, VoxelWorld } from "@/lib/world";
3	
4	export type SurfaceYAtFn = (x: number, z: number) => number;
5	
6	export function createSurfaceYAt(world: VoxelWorld): SurfaceYAtFn {
7	  return (x: number, z: number) => {
8	    const ix = Math.floor(x);
9	    const iz = Math.floor(z);
10	    if (ix < 0 || iz < 0 || ix >= world.sizeX || iz >= world.sizeZ) return 1;
11	    return world.highestSolidY(ix, iz) + 1;
12	  };
13	}
14	
15	/**
16	 * Deterministic spiral search for a safe spawn column: solid dry floor, two
17	 * air blocks for the body, gently sloped neighbors, preferring Plains. Falls
18	 * back to any biome, then to the search center.
19	 */
20	export function findSpawnOnLand(world: VoxelWorld, centerX: number, centerZ: number, seekPlains = true): { x: number; y: number; z: number } {
21	  const isGoodSpawn = (x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean => {
22	    const top = world.get(x, y - 1, z);
23	    const atBody = world.get(x, y, z);
24	    const atHead = world.get(x, y + 1, z);
25	    if (!world.isSolid(x, y - 1, z)) return false;
26	    if (atBody === BlockId.Water || atHead === BlockId.Water || top === BlockId.Water) return false;
27	    if (atBody !== BlockId.Air || atHead !== BlockId.Air) return false;
28	    if (seekPlains && world.getBiome(x, z) !== BiomeId.Plains) return false;
29	
30	    const h0 = world.highestSolidY(x + 1, z);
31	    const h1 = world.highestSolidY(x - 1, z);
32	    const h2 = world.highestSolidY(x, z + 1);
33	    const h3 = world.highestSolidY(x, z - 1);
34	    const ny = y - 1;
35	    return Math.abs(h0 - ny) <= 2 && Math.abs(h1 - ny) <= 2 && Math.abs(h2 - ny) <= 2 && Math.abs(h3 - ny) <= 2;
36	  };
37	
38	  const maxRadius = Math.min(300, Math.floor(Math.min(world.sizeX, world.sizeZ) * 0.4));
39	  for (let radius = 0; radius <= maxRadius; radius += 2) {
40	    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i += 1) {
41	      const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / 32;
42	      const x = Math.max(5, Math.min(world.sizeX - 6, Math.floor(centerX + Math.cos(angle) * radius)));
43	      const z = Math.max(5, Math.min(world.sizeZ - 6, Math.floor(centerZ + Math.sin(angle) * radius)));
44	      const topY = world.highestSolidY(x, z);
45	      const y = topY + 1;
46	      if (isGoodSpawn(x, y, z)) return { x, y, z };
47	    }
48	  }
49	
50	  if (seekPlains) return findSpawnOnLand(world, centerX, centerZ, false);
51	
52	  const topY = world.highestSolidY(centerX, centerZ);
53	  return { x: centerX, y: topY + 1, z: centerZ };
54	}
55	
56	/** True when the surface position is dry: not a flooded column (seabed/lake floor). */
57	function isDrySurface(world: VoxelWorld, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
58	  return world.get(Math.floor(x), y, Math.floor(z)) !== BlockId.Water;
59	}
60	
61	export function randomLandPoint(world: VoxelWorld, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn, rng: () => number = Math.random): THREE.Vector3 {
62	  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {
63	    const x = 10 + rng() * (world.sizeX - 20);
64	    const z = 10 + rng() * (world.sizeZ - 20);
65	    const y = surfaceYAt(x, z);
66	    if (y > 2 && isDrySurface(world, x, y, z)) return new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z);
67	  }
68	  return new THREE.Vector3(world.sizeX / 2, 12, world.sizeZ / 2);
69	}
70	
71	export function randomLandPointNear(
72	  world: VoxelWorld,
73	  surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn,
74	  centerX: number,
75	  centerZ: number,
76	  radius: number,
77	  rng: () => number = Math.random
78	): THREE.Vector3 {
79	  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i += 1) {
80	    const x = centerX + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;
81	    const z = centerZ + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;
82	    const clampedX = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeX - 10, x));
83	    const clampedZ = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeZ - 10, z));
84	    const y = surfaceYAt(clampedX, clampedZ);
85	    if (y > 2 && isDrySurface(world, clampedX, y, clampedZ)) return new THREE.Vector3(clampedX, y, clampedZ);
86	  }
87	  return randomLandPoint(world, surfaceYAt, rng);
88	}
89

1	import { BiomeId, BlockId, WORLD_SIZE_X, WORLD_SIZE_Y, WORLD_SIZE_Z } from "./blocks";
2	
3	/**
4	 * Voxel data store plus cheap world queries. Terrain generation lives in
5	 * generation.ts, meshing in meshing.ts, raycast/collision in queries.ts.
6	 *
7	 * ⚠ Save-format invariants: saves address voxels through index(), and getBiome()
8	 * feeds terrain generation. Changing either breaks every existing save — see
9	 * generation.test.ts.
10	 */
11	export class VoxelWorld {
12	  readonly sizeX: number;
13	  readonly sizeY: number;
14	  readonly sizeZ: number;
15	  readonly seed: number;
16	  readonly blocks: Uint8Array;
17	
18	  constructor(sizeX = WORLD_SIZE_X, sizeY = WORLD_SIZE_Y, sizeZ = WORLD_SIZE_Z, seed = 1337) {
19	    this.sizeX = sizeX;
20	    this.sizeY = sizeY;
21	    this.sizeZ = sizeZ;
22	    this.seed = seed;
23	    this.blocks = new Uint8Array(sizeX * sizeY * sizeZ);
24	  }
25	
26	  index(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
27	    return x + z * this.sizeX + y * this.sizeX * this.sizeZ;
28	  }
29	
30	  inBounds(x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
31	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && z >= 0 && x < this.sizeX && y < this.sizeY && z < this.sizeZ;
32	  }
33	
34	  get(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
35	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y, z)) return BlockId.Air;
36	    return this.blocks[this.index(x, y, z)];
37	  }
38	
39	  set(x: number, y: number, z: number, block: BlockId): void {
40	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y, z)) return;
41	    this.blocks[this.index(x, y, z)] = block;
42	  }
43	
44	  isSolid(x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
45	    const block = this.get(x, y, z);
46	    return block !== BlockId.Air && block !== BlockId.Water;
47	  }
48	
49	  highestSolidY(x: number, z: number): number {
50	    for (let y = this.sizeY - 1; y >= 0; y -= 1) {
51	      if (this.isSolid(x, y, z)) return y;
52	    }
53	    return 0;
54	  }
55	
56	  getBiome(x: number, z: number): BiomeId {
57	    // Two incommensurate, direction-rotated sine octaves per field, with
58	    // dominant wavelengths of ~250-900 blocks: each field traverses several
59	    // cycles across the 512-block map, so every world gets coherent patches
60	    // of all five biomes (~60-150 block scale).
61	    const s = this.seed * 0.007;
62	    const temp = Math.sin(x * 0.013 + z * 0.006 + s * 1.3) * 0.6 + Math.sin(x * 0.029 - z * 0.017 + s * 2.4) * 0.4;
63	    const moisture = Math.sin(x * 0.007 - z * 0.012 + s * 0.8) * 0.6 + Math.sin(x * 0.019 + z * 0.023 - s * 1.6) * 0.4;
64	    const continental = Math.sin(x * 0.008 + z * 0.01 + s * 2.1) * 0.55 + Math.sin(x * 0.014 - z * 0.009 - s * 1.7) * 0.45;
65	    const ridge = Math.sin(x * 0.024 + z * 0.02 + s) * 0.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.016 - z * 0.024 - s) * 0.5;
66	
67	    if (continental < -0.52) return BiomeId.Ocean;
68	    if ((continental > 0.5 && ridge > 0) || ridge > 0.8) return BiomeId.Mountains;
69	    if (temp > 0.2 && moisture < -0.12) return BiomeId.Desert;
70	    if (moisture > 0.18) return BiomeId.Forest;
71	    return BiomeId.Plains;
72	  }
73	}
74	
```

64	/**
65	 * Body-center height above the ground for a mob kind. Mirrors the geometry
66	 * math in createMobModel so the headless simulation needs no Three.js meshes.
67	 */
68	export function mobHalfHeight(kind: MobKind): number {
69	  const bodyHeight = MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs[5][1];
70	  const legHeight = Math.max(0.3, bodyHeight * 0.56);
71	  return Math.max(bodyHeight, legHeight) * 0.5 + 0.2;
72	}

74	/** Builds the Three.js model for a mob kind (renderer side). */
75	export function createMobModelForKind(kind: MobKind) {
76	  return createMobModel(...MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs);
77	}

1	import { MAX_STACK_SIZE } from "@/lib/game/config";
2	import { ARMOR_SLOTS, createEmptySlot, createSlot, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
3	import type { EquippedArmor, InventorySlot, Recipe } from "@/lib/game/types";
4	
5	/**
6	 * Pure inventory slot algebra. Every mutation returns a NEW slots array, or
7	 * null when the operation does not apply — callers keep the previous array on
8	 * null, so reference equality doubles as a change signal for React.
9	 */
10	
11	export function cloneSlots(slots: InventorySlot[]): InventorySlot[] {
12	  return slots.map((slot) => ({ ...slot }));
13	}
14	
15	export function countsById(slots: InventorySlot[]): Map<string, number> {
16	  const byId = new Map<string, number>();
17	  for (const slot of slots) {
18	    if (!slot.id || slot.count <= 0) continue;
19	    byId.set(slot.id, (byId.get(slot.id) ?? 0) + slot.count);
20	  }
21	  return byId;
22	}
23	
24	/**
25	 * Adds (delta > 0) or removes (delta < 0) items. Removal is all-or-nothing:
26	 * if the inventory holds fewer than |delta|, nothing is removed and null is
27	 * returned. Addition fills existing stacks, then empty slots; whatever does
28	 * not fit is dropped (a full inventory loses surplus block drops).
29	 */
30	export function adjustSlotCount(slots: InventorySlot[], slotId: string, delta: number, preferredIndex?: number): InventorySlot[] | null {
31	  if (!slotId || delta === 0) return null;
32	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
33	  let remaining = Math.abs(delta);
34	
35	  if (delta < 0) {
36	    const consumeFromIndex = (index: number) => {
37	      if (remaining <= 0) return;
38	      if (index < 0 || index >= next.length) return;
39	      const slot = next[index];
40	      if (slot.id !== slotId || slot.count <= 0) return;
41	      const take = Math.min(remaining, slot.count);
42	      slot.count -= take;
43	      remaining -= take;
44	      if (slot.count <= 0) next[index] = createEmptySlot();
45	    };
46	
47	    if (typeof preferredIndex === "number") consumeFromIndex(preferredIndex);
48	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) consumeFromIndex(i);
49	    if (remaining > 0) return null;
50	    return next;
51	  }
52	
53	  if (!ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[slotId]) return null;
54	
55	  const fillIndex = (index: number) => {
56	    if (remaining <= 0) return;
57	    if (index < 0 || index >= next.length) return;
58	    const slot = next[index];
59	    if (slot.id !== slotId || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) return;
60	    const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count);
```

191	}

193	/**
194	 * All-or-nothing insert of whole slot objects into `inventory` (used when a
195	 * broken chest spills its contents). Stackable items merge into existing stacks
196	 * then empty slots; durability items (tools/weapons/armor) take a whole empty
197	 * slot each so each piece keeps its own `durability`. Returns a new array, or
198	 * null if everything does not fit — the caller then leaves the inventory (and
199	 * the chest) untouched.
200	 */
201	export function tryInsertSlots(inventory: InventorySlot[], incoming: InventorySlot[]): InventorySlot[] | null {
202	  const next = cloneSlots(inventory);
203	
204	  const insertStackable = (id: string, count: number): boolean => {
205	    let remaining = count;
206	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
207	      const slot = next[i];
208	      if (slot.id !== id || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) continue;
209	      const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count);
210	      slot.count += add;
211	      remaining -= add;
212	    }
213	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
214	      if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;
215	      const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE);
216	      next[i] = createSlot(id, add);
217	      remaining -= add;
218	    }
219	    return remaining === 0;
220	  };
221	
222	  const insertDurable = (slot: InventorySlot): boolean => {
223	    let remaining = slot.count;
224	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
225	      if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;
226	      next[i] = { ...slot, count: 1 };
227	      remaining -= 1;
228	    }
229	    return remaining === 0;
230	  };
231	
232	  for (const slot of incoming) {
233	    if (!slot.id || slot.count <= 0) continue;
234	    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[slot.id];
235	    if (!def) continue; // unknown ids carry no data to preserve
236	    const placed = def.maxDurability ? insertDurable(slot) : insertStackable(slot.id, slot.count);
237	    if (!placed) return null;
238	  }
239	
240	  return next;
241	}
242	
243	/** Toggles the armor piece at `index` in its armor slot. */
244	export function toggleEquipArmor(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor, index: number): EquippedArmor | null {
245	  if (index < 0 || index >= slots.length) return null;
246	  const slot = slots[index];
247	  if (slot.kind !== "armor" || !slot.id || !slot.armorSlot || slot.count <= 0) return null;
248	  const next = { ...equipped };
249	  next[slot.armorSlot] = equipped[slot.armorSlot] === slot.id ? null : slot.id;

1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { BiomeId, BlockId, VoxelWorld, buildGeometryRegion, generateWorld } from "@/lib/world";

4	/**
5	 * Worldgen determinism characterization tests.
6	 *
7	 * Saves store only the seed plus block-change deltas, so world generation must
8	 * produce byte-identical output for a given seed forever. These digests pin the
9	 * current generator output.
10	 *
11	 * If a test here fails after a refactor, THE REFACTOR BROKE SAVE COMPATIBILITY —
12	 * fix the code, never the hash. Re-baselining is only legitimate for a deliberate,
13	 * CHANGELOG-flagged worldgen change, or for a Bun/JSC engine bump (the noise
14	 * functions use Math.sin, whose exact results are engine-defined; CI pins the Bun
15	 * version for this reason — see docs/testing.md).
16	 */
17	
18	function hashBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
19	  return new Bun.CryptoHasher("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");
20	}
21	
22	function makeWorld(sizeX: number, sizeY: number, sizeZ: number, seed: number): VoxelWorld {
23	  const world = new VoxelWorld(sizeX, sizeY, sizeZ, seed);
24	  generateWorld(world);
25	  return world;
26	}
27	
28	let fullWorldCache: VoxelWorld | null = null;
29	function fullWorld(): VoxelWorld {
30	  fullWorldCache ??= makeWorld(512, 150, 512, 1337);
31	  return fullWorldCache;
32	}

25	    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1); // min 1
26	  });
27	
28	  test("rng near 1 yields the maximum count", () => {
29	    const rng = () => 0.999;
30	    const drops = rollMobDrops("sheep", rng);
31	    const byId = new Map(drops.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));
32	    expect(byId.get("wool")).toBe(2); // max 2
33	    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1);
34	  });
35	
36	  test("a zero-minimum entry can drop nothing at the low roll", () => {
37	    // chicken feather is min 0; at rng 0 the count floors to 0 and is omitted.
38	    const drops = rollMobDrops("chicken", () => 0);
39	    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "feather")).toBe(false);
40	    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "raw_chicken")).toBe(true);
41	  });
42	
43	  test("every kind has at least one drop entry", () => {
44	    for (const kind of ALL_KINDS) expect(MOB_DROPS[kind].length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
45	  });
46	});
47

  "new_string": "        if (block === BlockId.Chest) {\n          if ((x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82); // plank grain\n          if (face === \"top\" && y === 8) c = tone(base, 0.6); // lid seam\n          if (face === \"side\" && y === 7) c = tone(base, 0.58); // lid joint band\n          if (face === \"side\" && x >= 7 && x <= 8 && y >= 6 && y <= 9) c = tone([0.62, 0.64, 0.68], 0.9 + n * 0.2); // metal latch\n        }\n        if (block === BlockId.MossyCobblestone) {\n          if (n > 0.8) c = tone([0.5, 0.52, 0.5], 1.05); // pale cobble chunks\n          if (n < 0.32) c = tone([0.2, 0.4, 0.16], 0.85 + n * 0.5); // moss patches in the cracks\n        }\n        if (block === BlockId.Spawner) {\n          // A dark iron cage over a faint ember core — reads as a Minecraft spawner.\n          const bar = x % 5 === 0 || y % 5 === 0;\n          c = bar ? tone([0.32, 0.34, 0.4], 0.85 + n * 0.25) : tone([0.5, 0.16, 0.12], 0.5 + n * 0.6);\n        }"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts",
  "content": "import type { MobDrop } from \"@/lib/game/mobLoot\";\n\n/**\n * Dungeon chest loot, mirroring the mob/block drop-table pattern\n * (`{ itemId, min, max, chance? }` entries rolled for an inclusive count).\n *\n * A chest rolls a tier first: most are `common` (staples — food, low ores, a\n * starter tool), a minority are `rare` and additionally roll the standout\n * payoff table (high ores, mid-tier gear). Rare chests still get the common\n * roll on top, so they are never *worse* than a common chest. Item ids must\n * exist in ITEM_DEFS — dungeonLoot.test.ts enforces that.\n */\nexport type LootEntry = MobDrop;\nexport type LootTier = \"common\" | \"rare\";\n\n/** Probability a chest rolls the rare tier (on top of the common roll). */\nexport const RARE_CHEST_CHANCE = 0.25;\n\nexport const DUNGEON_LOOT: Record<LootTier, LootEntry[]> = {\n  common: [\n    // Bone always drops, so every dungeon chest holds at least something.\n    { itemId: \"bone\", min: 1, max: 3 },\n    { itemId: \"bread\", min: 1, max: 3, chance: 0.8 },\n    { itemId: \"cooked_chicken\", min: 1, max: 2, chance: 0.5 },\n    { itemId: \"string\", min: 0, max: 2 },\n    { itemId: \"seeds\", min: 0, max: 2 },\n    { itemId: \"sliver_ore\", min: 1, max: 3, chance: 0.4 },\n    { itemId: \"gold_ore\", min: 1, max: 3, chance: 0.4 },\n    { itemId: \"stone_pickaxe\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.25 }\n  ],\n  rare: [\n    { itemId: \"diamond_ore\", min: 1, max: 2, chance: 0.3 },\n    { itemId: \"sapphire_ore\", min: 1, max: 2, chance: 0.35 },\n    { itemId: \"ruby_sword\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.15 },\n    { itemId: \"sapphire_sword\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.15 },\n    { itemId: \"helmet\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.2 },\n    { itemId: \"chestplate\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.2 },\n    { itemId: \"diamond_pickaxe\", min: 1, max: 1, chance: 0.08 }\n  ]\n};\n\n/** Clamps an rng sample into [0, 1) so a pathological injected rng can't over-roll. */\nconst clampUnit = (v: number): number => Math.min(1 - Number.EPSILON, Math.max(0, v));\n\n/**\n * A small mulberry32 PRNG. Dungeon chests are filled lazily on first open, so\n * the loot must be reproducible from a stable seed (the world seed mixed with\n * the chest's voxel index) until the chest is actually opened — otherwise the\n * same chest would roll different loot before vs after a reload.\n */\nexport function seededRng(seed: number): () => number {\n  let t = seed >>> 0;\n  return () => {\n    t += 0x6d2b79f5;\n    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);\n    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);\n    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  };\n}\n\nfunction rollEntries(entries: LootEntry[], rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {\n  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    if (entry.chance !== undefined && clampUnit(rng()) >= entry.chance) continue;\n    const span = entry.max - entry.min + 1;\n    const count = entry.min + Math.floor(clampUnit(rng()) * span);\n    if (count > 0) drops.push({ itemId: entry.itemId, count });\n  }\n  return drops;\n}\n\n/**\n * Rolls one dungeon chest. Consumes one rng sample for the tier, then rolls the\n * common table (always) plus the rare table when the chest is rare. `rng` is\n * injectable so tests get deterministic loot (and the engine seeds it per chest).\n */\nexport function rollDungeonLoot(rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {\n  const rare = clampUnit(rng()) < RARE_CHEST_CHANCE;\n  const drops = rare ? rollEntries(DUNGEON_LOOT.rare, rng) : [];\n  drops.push(...rollEntries(DUNGEON_LOOT.common, rng));\n  return drops;\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/dungeonLoot.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from \"@/lib/game/items\";\nimport { DUNGEON_LOOT, rollDungeonLoot, seededRng, type LootTier } from \"@/lib/game/dungeonLoot\";\n\nconst ALL_TIERS = Object.keys(DUNGEON_LOOT) as LootTier[];\n\ndescribe(\"dungeon loot tables\", () => {\n  test(\"every loot entry references an existing item with a sane range\", () => {\n    for (const tier of ALL_TIERS) {\n      for (const entry of DUNGEON_LOOT[tier]) {\n        expect(ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[entry.itemId]).toBeDefined();\n        expect(entry.min).toBeLessThanOrEqual(entry.max);\n        expect(entry.min).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n        if (entry.chance !== undefined) {\n          expect(entry.chance).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n          expect(entry.chance).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  });\n\n  test(\"seededRng is deterministic for a given seed\", () => {\n    const a = seededRng(12345);\n    const b = seededRng(12345);\n    const c = seededRng(99999);\n    const seqA = [a(), a(), a(), a()];\n    const seqB = [b(), b(), b(), b()];\n    expect(seqA).toEqual(seqB);\n    expect([c(), c(), c(), c()]).not.toEqual(seqA);\n  });\n\n  test(\"rolling the same seed yields identical loot (reproducible until opened)\", () => {\n    const loot1 = rollDungeonLoot(seededRng(0x1234));\n    const loot2 = rollDungeonLoot(seededRng(0x1234));\n    expect(loot1).toEqual(loot2);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a low roll opens the rare tier and yields minimum counts\", () => {\n    // rng() === 0: tier roll (0 < RARE_CHEST_CHANCE) is rare; every chance entry\n    // passes (0 >= chance is false) and counts floor to their minimum.\n    const loot = rollDungeonLoot(() => 0);\n    const byId = new Map(loot.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));\n    expect(byId.get(\"diamond_ore\")).toBe(1); // rare entry, min 1\n    expect(byId.get(\"bone\")).toBe(1); // common guaranteed entry, min 1\n  });\n\n  test(\"a high roll stays common and drops only the guaranteed entries\", () => {\n    // rng() === 0.999: not rare, and every chance-gated entry is skipped, so\n    // only the always-on entries (bone/string/seeds) can appear.\n    const loot = rollDungeonLoot(() => 0.999);\n    expect(loot.some((d) => d.itemId === \"bread\")).toBe(false); // chance-gated, skipped\n    expect(loot.some((d) => d.itemId === \"diamond_ore\")).toBe(false); // not the rare tier\n    expect(loot.some((d) => d.itemId === \"bone\")).toBe(true); // always drops\n  });\n\n  test(\"counts are always positive\", () => {\n    const rng = seededRng(0xbeef);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i += 1) {\n      for (const drop of rollDungeonLoot(rng)) {\n        expect(drop.count).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/world/generation.ts",
  "old_string": "  for (let x = cx - half - 1; x <= cx + half + 1; x += 1) {\n    for (let z = cz - half - 1; z <= cz + half + 1; z += 1) world.set(x, floorY + 4, z, BlockId.Planks);\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "  for (let x = cx - half - 1; x <= cx + half + 1; x += 1) {\n    for (let z = cz - half - 1; z <= cz + half + 1; z += 1) world.set(x, floorY + 4, z, BlockId.Planks);\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Dungeons ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Small underground cobblestone rooms with pre-fillable loot chests and a mob\n// spawner. The chest CONTENTS are not generated here (the block-diff save can't\n// carry worldgen-placed entities), only the blocks — the engine fills chests\n// lazily on first open. To support that, the engine re-derives the chest and\n// spawner voxel indices on load via collectDungeonSites(), which replays this\n// exact placement math. Two invariants keep build and derive in lockstep:\n//   1. A dedicated PRNG seeded only from world.seed (independent of the shared\n//      generation stream), and every dungeon draws a fixed number of values up\n//      front so the stream stays aligned whether or not the dungeon is placed.\n//   2. Placement validates against terrainTopY() — a seed-pure surface estimate\n//      — never highestSolidY(), which a cave or a player edit could shift.\n\ntype DungeonSink = { chest: (idx: number) => void; spawner: (idx: number) => void };\n\nconst NOOP_DUNGEON_SINK: DungeonSink = { chest: () => {}, spawner: () => {} };\n\n/** A PRNG seeded only from the world seed, decoupled from the main gen stream. */\nfunction dungeonRand(seed: number): () => number {\n  let t = (seed ^ 0x9e3779b9) >>> 0;\n  return () => {\n    t += 0x6d2b79f5;\n    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);\n    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);\n    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * The generation-time terrain surface at (x,z), computed purely from the seed\n * (mirrors the topY math in generateTerrain). Unlike highestSolidY it reads no\n * blocks, so it is identical at generation and at load — the property that lets\n * collectDungeonSites reproduce the exact dungeon layout.\n */\nfunction terrainTopY(world: VoxelWorld, x: number, z: number): number {\n  const biome = world.getBiome(x, z);\n  const noise = smoothNoise2D(x, z, world.seed);\n  const { baseHeight, noiseScale } = GEN.biomes[biome];\n  return Math.max(5, Math.min(world.sizeY - 5, Math.floor(baseHeight + noise * noiseScale)));\n}\n\nfunction buildDungeonRoom(world: VoxelWorld, cx: number, cz: number, floorY: number, secondChest: boolean, write: boolean, sink: DungeonSink): void {\n  const half = 3; // 7x7 outer, 5x5x4 hollow interior\n  if (write) {\n    for (let y = floorY - 1; y <= floorY + 4; y += 1) {\n      for (let x = cx - half; x <= cx + half; x += 1) {\n        for (let z = cz - half; z <= cz + half; z += 1) {\n          if (!world.inBounds(x, y, z)) continue;\n          const interior = x > cx - half && x < cx + half && z > cz - half && z < cz + half && y >= floorY && y <= floorY + 3;\n          if (interior) {\n            world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Air);\n          } else {\n            const mossy = hash2D(x * 1.7 + y * 0.9, z * 2.3 - y * 0.6) > 0.6;\n            world.set(x, y, z, mossy ? BlockId.MossyCobblestone : BlockId.Cobblestone);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    world.set(cx - 2, floorY, cz - 2, BlockId.Chest);\n    if (secondChest) world.set(cx + 2, floorY, cz + 2, BlockId.Chest);\n    world.set(cx, floorY, cz, BlockId.Spawner);\n  }\n  sink.chest(world.index(cx - 2, floorY, cz - 2));\n  if (secondChest) sink.chest(world.index(cx + 2, floorY, cz + 2));\n  sink.spawner(world.index(cx, floorY, cz));\n}\n\nfunction buildDungeons(world: VoxelWorld, write: boolean, sink: DungeonSink): void {\n  const rand = dungeonRand(world.seed);\n  const centerX = world.sizeX / 2;\n  const centerZ = world.sizeZ / 2;\n  for (let i = 0; i < GEN.dungeonCount; i += 1) {\n    // Draw every random up front so the stream advances identically whether or\n    // not this dungeon turns out to be placeable.\n    const cx = 12 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 24));\n    const cz = 12 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 24));\n    const floorRoll = rand();\n    const secondChest = rand() < 0.6;\n\n    const surface = terrainTopY(world, cx, cz);\n    const minFloor = 7;\n    const maxFloor = surface - 7; // keep the ceiling >=3 below the surface\n    if (maxFloor < minFloor) continue;\n    // Keep dungeons clear of the spawn area so there's no free starter loot.\n    if (Math.hypot(cx - centerX, cz - centerZ) < 40) continue;\n\n    const floorY = minFloor + Math.floor(floorRoll * (maxFloor - minFloor + 1));\n    buildDungeonRoom(world, cx, cz, floorY, secondChest, write, sink);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction placeDungeons(world: VoxelWorld): void {\n  buildDungeons(world, true, NOOP_DUNGEON_SINK);\n}\n\nexport type DungeonSites = { chestIndices: number[]; spawnerIndices: number[] };\n\n/**\n * Re-derives the voxel indices of every dungeon chest and spawner WITHOUT\n * writing blocks, by replaying buildDungeons' placement math. The engine calls\n * this once after regenerating the world on load to rebuild the session-only\n * dungeon index sets that gate lazy loot fill and spawner activation.\n */\nexport function collectDungeonSites(world: VoxelWorld): DungeonSites {\n  const chestIndices: number[] = [];\n  const spawnerIndices: number[] = [];\n  buildDungeons(world, false, {\n    chest: (idx) => chestIndices.push(idx),\n    spawner: (idx) => spawnerIndices.push(idx)\n  });\n  return { chestIndices, spawnerIndices };\n}"
}

Rooms\nare speckled with mossy cobble for the Minecraft-dungeon look and kept\nclear of the spawn area so there's no free starter loot. This gives\nexploration a payoff and gives chests a found-loot purpose.\n\nThe chest CONTENTS can't live in the deterministic worldgen baseline\n(the block-diff save can't carry worldgen-placed entities), so the engine\nwill fill chests lazily on first open. That requires re-deriving the\nchest/spawner voxel indices on load: collectDungeonSites() replays the\nexact placement math without writing blocks. Two invariants keep the\nbuild and derive passes byte-for-byte aligned:\n\n  - a dedicated PRNG seeded only from world.seed (independent of the main\n    generation stream), with every dungeon drawing a fixed number of\n    values up front so the stream stays aligned whether or not the\n    dungeon is placeable;\n  - placement validates against terrainTopY() — a seed-pure surface\n    estimate — never highestSolidY(), which a cave or a player edit could\n    shift out from under the derive pass.\n\nWorldgen impact: this changes the deterministic world, so the worldgen\nSHA-256 digests and the in-region meshing geometry baseline are re-rolled\n(structural probes confirm the surface is untouched — dungeons are wholly\nunderground), and SAVE_KEY is bumped v5 -> v6 to discard old saves whose\nblock-diffs index against the old terrain. New tests assert dungeons\ngenerate (chests/spawners/mossy underground, clear of spawn) and that\ncollectDungeonSites reproduces every generated chest/spawner position.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",

}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/dungeon.ts",
  "content": "import { CHEST_SLOTS } from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { rollDungeonLoot, seededRng } from \"@/lib/game/dungeonLoot\";\nimport { createEmptySlot, createSlot } from \"@/lib/game/items\";\nimport { tryInsertSlots } from \"@/lib/game/inventory\";\nimport type { GameState } from \"../state\";\n\n/**\n * Fills a dungeon loot chest on its FIRST access (open or break) and marks it\n * looted. The loot is seeded from the world seed + the chest's voxel index, so\n * it is reproducible until the chest is accessed.\n *\n * Marking the index in the persisted `lootedDungeonChests` set — rather than\n * relying on the chest being non-empty — is what closes the re-roll exploit:\n * an emptied chest is dropped from the save's `blockEntities`, so without this\n * marker the lazy fill would run again on reload and hand out fresh loot.\n *\n * A no-op for player-placed chests (not in `dungeonChestIndices`) and for\n * dungeon chests already looted.\n */\nexport function fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted(state: GameState, idx: number): void {\n  if (!state.dungeonChestIndices.has(idx) || state.lootedDungeonChests.has(idx)) return;\n  const slots = state.containers.get(idx) ?? Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());\n  const loot = rollDungeonLoot(seededRng((state.world.seed ^ idx ^ 0x9e3779b1) >>> 0));\n  const incoming = loot.map((drop) => createSlot(drop.itemId, drop.count));\n  state.containers.set(idx, tryInsertSlots(slots, incoming) ?? slots);\n  state.lootedDungeonChests.add(idx);\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "/** Opens the chest at (x,y,z) in the inventory panel, creating its (lazy) empty store. */\nfunction interactChest(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {\n  const idx = state.world.index(x, y, z);\n  if (!state.containers.has(idx)) {\n    state.containers.set(\n      idx,\n      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())\n    );\n  }\n  state.openContainerIndex = idx;",
  "new_string": "/** Opens the chest at (x,y,z) in the inventory panel, creating its (lazy) empty store. */\nfunction interactChest(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {\n  const idx = state.world.index(x, y, z);\n  if (!state.containers.has(idx)) {\n    state.containers.set(\n      idx,\n      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())\n    );\n  }\n  // A worldgen dungeon chest rolls its loot here, on first open (then never again).\n  fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted(state, idx);\n  state.openContainerIndex = idx;"
}

1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";

21	function memoryStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {
22	  const data = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
23	  return {
24	    get length() {
25	      return data.size;
26	    },
27	    clear: () => data.clear(),
28	    getItem: (key: string) => data.get(key) ?? null,
29	    key: (index: number) => [...data.keys()][index] ?? null,
30	    removeItem: (key: string) => void data.delete(key),
31	    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void data.set(key, value)
32	  };
33	}
34	
35	const KEY = "test_save";
36	
37	function sampleSave(): SaveData {
38	  return {
39	    version: 4,
40	    seed: 1337,
41	    changes: [
42	      [42, 0],
43	      [99, 3]
44	    ],
45	    inventorySlots: [
46	      { id: "dirt", count: 12 },
47	      { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1, durability: 35 },
48	      { id: null, count: 0 }
49	    ],
50	    equippedArmor: { helmet: "helmet" },
51	    selectedSlot: 2,
52	    player: { x: 100.5, y: 48, z: 200.25 },
53	    dayClock: 123.5,
54	    hearts: 14,
55	    hunger: 9,
56	    spawnPoint: { x: 10, y: 40, z: 20 }
57	  };
58	}
59	
60	describe("save round-trip", () => {
61	  test("writeSave then readSave preserves every field", () => {

63	    writeSave(KEY, sampleSave(), storage);
64	    expect(readSave(KEY, storage)).toEqual(sampleSave());
65	  });

73	      selectedSlot: 0,
74	      player: { x: 1, y: 2, z: 3 }

83	});

85	describe("v1 to v2 migration", () => {

224	    const empty = [createEmptySlot(), createEmptySlot()];
225	    const out = serializeContainers(
226	      new Map<number, InventorySlot[]>([
227	        [100, full],
228	        [200, empty]
229	      ])
230	    );
231	    expect(out).toHaveLength(1);
232	    expect(out[0].index).toBe(100);
233	    expect(out[0].slots[0]).toEqual({ id: "dirt", count: 5, durability: undefined });
234	  });
235	
236	  test("a chest round-trips through save with durability preserved", () => {
237	    const slots = [createSlot("dirt", 5), { ...createSlot("diamond_sword", 1), durability: 200 }];
238	    const save: SaveData = { ...sampleSave(), blockEntities: serializeContainers(new Map([[100, slots]])) };
239	    const storage = memoryStorage();
240	    writeSave(KEY, save, storage);
241	    const restored = readContainers(readSave(KEY, storage)!);
242	    expect(restored).toHaveLength(1);
243	    expect(restored[0].index).toBe(100);
244	    expect(restored[0].slots).toHaveLength(27); // padded to CHEST_SLOTS
245	    expect(restored[0].slots[0].id).toBe("dirt");
246	    expect(restored[0].slots[1].durability).toBe(200);
247	  });
248	
249	  test("readContainers drops unknown ids and malformed entries", () => {
250	    const save: SaveData = {
251	      ...sampleSave(),
252	      blockEntities: [
253	        {
254	          index: 100,
255	          slots: [
256	            { id: "no_such_item", count: 3 },
257	            { id: "stone", count: 2 }
258	          ]
259	        },
260	        { index: Number.NaN, slots: [] }
261	      ]
262	    };
263	    const restored = readContainers(save);
264	    expect(restored).toHaveLength(1); // NaN index dropped
265	    expect(restored[0].slots[0].id).toBeNull(); // unknown id -> empty
266	    expect(restored[0].slots[1].id).toBe("stone");
267	  });
268	});
269	
270	describe("stat restoration helpers", () => {
271	  const base = sampleSave();
272	
273	  test("missing fields restore as null so the engine keeps its defaults", () => {
274	    const bare: SaveData = { ...base, dayClock: undefined, hearts: undefined, hunger: undefined, spawnPoint: undefined };
275	    expect(restoreDayClock(bare)).toBeNull();
276	    expect(restoreHearts(bare)).toBeNull();
277	    expect(restoreHungerLevel(bare)).toBeNull();
278	    expect(restoreSpawnPoint(bare)).toBeNull();
279	  });
280	
281	  test("out-of-range values are clamped", () => {
282	    expect(restoreHearts({ ...base, hearts: 999 })).toBe(MAX_HEARTS);
283	    expect(restoreHearts({ ...base, hearts: 0 })).toBe(1);
284	    expect(restoreHungerLevel({ ...base, hunger: -5 })).toBe(0);
285	    expect(restoreHungerLevel({ ...base, hunger: 999 })).toBe(MAX_HUNGER);
286	    expect(restoreDayClock({ ...base, dayClock: -1 })).toBeNull();
287	    expect(restoreDayClock({ ...base, dayClock: Number.NaN })).toBeNull();
288	  });

291	    expect(restoreSpawnPoint({ ...base, spawnPoint: { x: 5.9, y: 40.2, z: 20.7 } })).toEqual({ x: 5, y: 40, z: 20 });
292	    expect(restoreSpawnPoint({ ...base, spawnPoint: null })).toBeNull();
293	  });
294	});

202	describe("chests", () => {
203	  /** Settles the player, centers it in its cell, and aims straight down at the floor block. */
204	  function aimDownAtFloor(engine: GameEngine): { x: number; y: number; z: number; idx: number } {
205	    calmDaytime(engine);
206	    engine.state.mobs = [];
207	    run(engine, 1);
208	    const { state } = engine;
209	    const x = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);
210	    const y = Math.floor(state.player.position.y) - 1;
211	    const z = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);
212	    state.player.position.x = x + 0.5;
213	    state.player.position.z = z + 0.5;
214	    state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;
215	    return { x, y, z, idx: state.world.index(x, y, z) };
216	  }
217

388	/** Decodes a voxel index back to [x, y, z] for the active world. */
389	function indexToXYZ(state: GameEngine["state"], idx: number): [number, number, number] {
390	  const layer = state.world.sizeX * state.world.sizeZ;
391	  const y = Math.floor(idx / layer);
392	  const rem = idx - y * layer;
393	  const z = Math.floor(rem / state.world.sizeX);
394	  const x = rem - z * state.world.sizeX;
395	  return [x, y, z];
396	}

24	/**
25	 * Headless simulation tests: the engine boots a real generated world and runs
26	 * real frames without React, Three.js rendering, or a DOM.
27	 */
28	
29	function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
30	  let t = seed >>> 0;
31	  return () => {
32	    t += 0x6d2b79f5;
33	    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
34	    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
35	    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
36	  };
37	}
38	
39	function makeEngine(save: ReturnType<GameEngine["serialize"]> | null = null): GameEngine {
40	  return new GameEngine({ save, seed: 1337, rng: mulberry32(42), worldSize: { x: 64, y: 150, z: 64 } });
41	}
42	
43	function input(overrides: Partial<{ keys: string[]; capsActive: boolean; leftMouseHeld: boolean; pointerLocked: boolean }> = {}): FrameInput {
44	  return {
45	    keys: new Set(overrides.keys ?? []),
46	    capsActive: overrides.capsActive ?? false,
47	    leftMouseHeld: overrides.leftMouseHeld ?? false,
48	    pointerLocked: overrides.pointerLocked ?? false
49	  };
50	}
51	
52	function run(engine: GameEngine, seconds: number, frame: FrameInput = input()): void {
53	  const dt = 1 / 60;
54	  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += dt) engine.step(dt, frame);
55	}
56	
57	/**
58	 * The world boots at dawn (daylight 0.05), so the initial hostiles aggro
59	 * immediately. Tests about other mechanics clear them and move to midday.
60	 */
61	function calmDaytime(engine: GameEngine): void {
62	  engine.state.mobs = engine.state.mobs.filter((mob) => !mob.hostile);
63	  engine.state.dayClock = 60;
64	}
65

73	    expect(countsById(state.inventory).get("wood")).toBe(64);
74	    expect(engine.getSnapshot().hearts).toBe(MAX_HEARTS);
75	    expect(engine.getSnapshot().passiveCount).toBe(14);

{
  "command": "git add lib/game/types.ts lib/game/save.ts lib/game/save.test.ts lib/game/engine/state.ts lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts lib/game/engine/systems/dungeon.ts lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts e2e/smoke.e2e.ts && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nfeat: save v5 + dungeon-chest loot persistence\n\nFill dungeon loot chests lazily on first access and persist which ones\nhave been opened, closing the re-roll exploit the deferred fill would\notherwise create.\n\nDungeon chests are placed by deterministic worldgen, so their contents\ncan't live in the block-diff save baseline. Filling them eagerly would\nmean a chest the player empties is dropped from the save (serializeContainers\nskips empty chests) and then re-rolls fresh loot on the next reload —\ninfinite loot. The fix decouples \"has this been looted\" from \"is this\nchest currently non-empty\":\n\n- A session-only `dungeonChestIndices`/`dungeonSpawnerIndices` Set, rebuilt\n  each load from the seed via collectDungeonSites (the world is regenerated\n  deterministically, so these match generation).\n- A persisted `lootedDungeonChests` Set (save format v5, additive\n  `lootedChests` field; migrateSaveV4toV5 is a pure version bump).\n- fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted (shared by interact-open and break-spill) rolls\n  loot seeded from world.seed ^ voxelIndex on the FIRST access and marks the\n  index looted. Marked-on-access, not on-empty, so an emptied-then-reloaded\n  chest stays looted and never re-rolls.\n\nTests cover the v4→v5 migration, lootedChests round-trip, first-access fill,\nfill-on-break, and the reload-after-emptying exploit case. The e2e save-key\nliteral follows config's SAVE_KEY bump to v6.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit slice 4: save v5 + loot persistence"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts",
  "old_string": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { HOSTILE_CAP, HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT, HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS, RENDER_RADIUS } from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight } from \"@/lib/game/mobs\";\nimport { randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from \"@/lib/game/spawn\";\nimport type { MobKind } from \"@/lib/game/types\";\nimport type { GameState, MobState } from \"../state\";\n\nexport type SpawnGroupArgs = {\n  kind: MobKind;\n  hostile: boolean;\n  count: number;\n  centerX: number;\n  centerZ: number;\n  radius: number;\n};\n\nexport function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {\n  const template = MOB_TEMPLATES[args.kind];\n  const halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(args.kind);\n  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {\n    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng);\n    const mob: MobState = {\n      id: state.nextMobId,\n      kind: args.kind,\n      hostile: args.hostile,\n      hp: template.hp,\n      position: new THREE.Vector3(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y + halfHeight, spawnPos.z),\n      direction: new THREE.Vector3(rng() - 0.5, 0, rng() - 0.5).normalize(),\n      yaw: 0,\n      turnTimer: 1 + rng() * 3,\n      speed: template.speed,\n      moveSpeed: template.speed,\n      detectRange: template.detectRange,\n      attackDamage: template.attackDamage,\n      attackCooldown: template.attackCooldown,\n      attackTimer: rng(),\n      halfHeight,\n      bobSeed: rng() * 10,\n      fedTimer: 0,\n      ageTimer: 0\n    };\n    state.nextMobId += 1;\n    state.mobs.push(mob);\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { BlockId } from \"@/lib/world\";\nimport {\n  HOSTILE_CAP,\n  HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT,\n  HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS,\n  RENDER_RADIUS,\n  SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS,\n  SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS,\n  SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP\n} from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight } from \"@/lib/game/mobs\";\nimport { randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from \"@/lib/game/spawn\";\nimport type { MobKind } from \"@/lib/game/types\";\nimport type { EmitGameEvent, GameState, MobState } from \"../state\";\n\nexport type SpawnGroupArgs = {\n  kind: MobKind;\n  hostile: boolean;\n  count: number;\n  centerX: number;\n  centerZ: number;\n  radius: number;\n};\n\n/** Adds one mob standing with its feet at (x, y, z) (y is the ground, not the body center). */\nfunction pushMob(state: GameState, kind: MobKind, hostile: boolean, x: number, y: number, z: number, rng: () => number): void {\n  const template = MOB_TEMPLATES[kind];\n  const halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(kind);\n  const mob: MobState = {\n    id: state.nextMobId,\n    kind,\n    hostile,\n    hp: template.hp,\n    position: new THREE.Vector3(x, y + halfHeight, z),\n    direction: new THREE.Vector3(rng() - 0.5, 0, rng() - 0.5).normalize(),\n    yaw: 0,\n    turnTimer: 1 + rng() * 3,\n    speed: template.speed,\n    moveSpeed: template.speed,\n    detectRange: template.detectRange,\n    attackDamage: template.attackDamage,\n    attackCooldown: template.attackCooldown,\n    attackTimer: rng(),\n    halfHeight,\n    bobSeed: rng() * 10,\n    fedTimer: 0,\n    ageTimer: 0\n  };\n  state.nextMobId += 1;\n  state.mobs.push(mob);\n}\n\nexport function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {\n  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {\n    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng);\n    pushMob(state, args.kind, args.hostile, spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, spawnPos.z, rng);\n  }\n}"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts",
  "old_string": "  spawnMobGroup(\n    state,\n    {\n      kind,\n      hostile: true,\n      count: 1 + (rng() > 0.7 ? 1 : 0),\n      centerX: state.player.position.x,\n      centerZ: state.player.position.z,\n      radius: Math.max(26, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.85)\n    },\n    rng,\n    surfaceYAt\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "  spawnMobGroup(\n    state,\n    {\n      kind,\n      hostile: true,\n      count: 1 + (rng() > 0.7 ? 1 : 0),\n      centerX: state.player.position.x,\n      centerZ: state.player.position.z,\n      radius: Math.max(26, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.85)\n    },\n    rng,\n    surfaceYAt\n  );\n}\n\nconst SPAWNER_KINDS: ReadonlyArray<\"zombie\" | \"skeleton\" | \"spider\"> = [\"zombie\", \"skeleton\", \"spider\"];\n\n/**\n * Drips hostiles from dungeon spawners. On each interval, every intact spawner\n * with the player inside its activation radius spawns one hostile on the room\n * floor (feet at the spawner's own y, not the surface), bounded by a local\n * cluster cap and the shared global HOSTILE_CAP. Time-independent — dungeons\n * are dark. Spawner positions come from the session-derived index set, so\n * mining out a spawner block stops it (the world.get check below).\n */\nexport function tickSpawnerDirector(state: GameState, dt: number, rng: () => number, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {\n  if (state.dungeonSpawnerIndices.size === 0) return;\n  state.timers.spawnerTimer += dt;\n  if (state.timers.spawnerTimer < SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS) return;\n  state.timers.spawnerTimer = 0;\n\n  const countHostiles = () => state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0);\n  if (countHostiles() >= HOSTILE_CAP) return;\n\n  const { player, world } = state;\n  const layer = world.sizeX * world.sizeZ;\n  for (const idx of state.dungeonSpawnerIndices) {\n    const sy = Math.floor(idx / layer);\n    const rem = idx - sy * layer;\n    const sz = Math.floor(rem / world.sizeX);\n    const sx = rem - sz * world.sizeX;\n    if (world.get(sx, sy, sz) !== BlockId.Spawner) continue; // mined out → inert\n    if (Math.hypot(player.position.x - (sx + 0.5), player.position.y - (sy + 0.5), player.position.z - (sz + 0.5)) > SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS) continue;\n    if (countHostiles() >= HOSTILE_CAP) break;\n\n    const nearby = state.mobs.reduce(\n      (acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile && Math.hypot(mob.position.x - (sx + 0.5), mob.position.z - (sz + 0.5)) <= SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS ? 1 : 0),\n      0\n    );\n    if (nearby >= SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP) continue;\n\n    const kind = SPAWNER_KINDS[Math.floor(rng() * SPAWNER_KINDS.length)];\n    const jx = sx + 0.5 + (rng() * 2 - 1) * 1.2;\n    const jz = sz + 0.5 + (rng() * 2 - 1) * 1.2;\n    pushMob(state, kind, true, jx, sy, jz, rng);\n    emit({ type: \"mobSpawned\", kind, x: sx + 0.5, y: sy + 0.5, z: sz + 0.5 });\n  }\n}"
}

i += 1) {\n      tickSpawnerDirector(state, SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS, rng, (event) => {\n        if (event.type === \"mobSpawned\") spawnEvents += 1;\n      });\n    }\n\n    const hostiles = state.mobs.filter((mob) => mob.hostile).length;\n    expect(hostiles).toBe(SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP);\n    expect(spawnEvents).toBe(SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a spawner is inert when the player is out of range\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const { state } = engine;\n    const idx = placeSpawner(engine);\n    const [sx, sy, sz] = indexToXYZ(state, idx);\n    state.player.position.set(sx + 40, sy, sz + 40); // well outside the activation radius\n\n    const rng = mulberry32(7);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i += 1) tickSpawnerDirector(state, SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS, rng, () => {});\n\n    expect(state.mobs.length).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a mined-out spawner stops spawning\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const { state } = engine;\n    const idx = placeSpawner(engine);\n    const [sx, sy, sz] = indexToXYZ(state, idx);\n    state.player.position.set(sx + 1, sy, sz + 1);\n    state.blockChanges.set(sx, sy, sz, BlockId.Air); 1	# Save format & compatibility
2	
3	## Schema
4	
5	`SaveData` (version 4) in `lib/game/types.ts`:
6	
7	- world `seed`
8	- a block **diff** list — `changes: [blockIndex, blockId][]`; edits that revert to the generated baseline are pruned (player-placed crops, beds, furnaces, and chests ride this list — they are ordinary block edits)
9	- inventory slots with durability (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar)
10	- equipped armor
11	- selected hotbar slot (0–8)
12	- player position
13	- `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger` (all optional) — time of day and player stats, so sleeping through the night and a hurt/hungry player survive a reload
14	- `spawnPoint` (optional, `{x,y,z}` or null) — the bed respawn point; respawn falls back to a random land point when it is null or the block there is no longer a bed
15	- `blockEntities` (optional) — chest contents as `{ index, slots }[]`, keyed by the block's voxel index (the same index space as `changes`). Only **non-empty** chests are stored; an empty one carries no data and is recreated empty when opened. On load each entry is kept only if that index still holds a Chest block. This is the first per-block metadata in the format — `lib/game/save.ts` (`serializeContainers` / `readContainers`) handles it
16	
17	Stored in localStorage under `SAVE_KEY` (`minecraft_save_v5`, defined in `lib/game/config.ts`). Read/write and restore validation live in `lib/game/save.ts` (the `Storage` is injectable for tests); `GameEngine.serialize()` produces the save from live state, and `lib/game/engine/blockChanges.ts` maintains the block diff.
18	
19	Audio volume preferences live under a **separate** key (`minecraft_audio_v1`, `lib/game/audio/settings.ts`) and are not part of the world save or its versioning.
20	
21	### Version history
22	
23	- **v4** — adds optional `blockEntities` (chest contents). `migrateSaveV3toV4` is a pure version bump (absent → restores as no containers), so **`SAVE_KEY` is unchanged** (chests are never generated; worldgen and the index formula are untouched) and v1/v2/v3 saves load fine. `readSave` chains v1 → v2 → v3 → v4.
24	- **v3** — adds optional `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger`, and `spawnPoint`. `migrateSaveV2toV3` is a pure version bump (the new fields stay absent and restore as engine defaults), so **`SAVE_KEY` is unchanged** and v1/v2 saves load fine.
25	- **v2** — same fields as v1, reinterpreted for the 36-slot inventory and 9-slot hotbar.
26	- **v1** — 40 slots, 10-slot hotbar. Still accepted: `readSave` runs `migrateSaveV1toV2`, which packs non-empty slots in order, merges stackable items into earlier stacks, clamps `selectedSlot` to 0–8, and drops items that overflow the smaller inventory.
27	- Unknown future versions are rejected (treated as no save).
28	
29	## Autosave
30	
31	Every 15s via `setInterval`, plus on `beforeunload`.
32	
33	## Compatibility rules
34	
35	- Saves store **diffs against generated terrain**, so changing world generation or the voxel index formula (`x + z*sizeX + y*sizeX*sizeZ`) silently corrupts existing saves — bump `SAVE_KEY` when you do.
36	- Worldgen output is pinned by SHA-256 characterization tests in `lib/world/generation.test.ts`; they fail on any byte-level change. See [testing.md](testing.md) for the re-baseline policy. Caveat: the noise functions use `Math.sin`, whose exact results are engine-defined — the tests prove refactor purity on the pinned Bun version, not cross-browser save portability.
37	- Changing the save shape requires bumping the `version` field and handling (or discarding) old data. Round-trip tests live in `lib/game/save.test.ts` and `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`.
38	- Note save-format/worldgen impact in PRs.
39	
```

This is the first per-block metadata in the format — `lib/game/save.ts` (`serializeContainers` / `readContainers`) handles it\n\nStored in localStorage under `SAVE_KEY` (`minecraft_save_v5`, defined in `lib/game/config.ts`).",

Only **non-empty** chests are stored; an empty one carries no data and is recreated empty when opened. On load each entry is kept only if that index still holds a Chest block. This is the first per-block metadata in the format — `lib/game/save.ts` (`serializeContainers` / `readContainers`) handles it\n- `lootedChests` (optional) — voxel indices of dungeon loot chests the player has already opened or broken. Dungeon chests are placed by worldgen, so their contents are **not** in `changes`/`blockEntities` until accessed; the engine fills them lazily on first access (loot seeded from `world.seed ^ index`) and records the index here. Because an emptied chest drops out of `blockEntities`, this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a re-roll on reload. The session-only set of *which* indices are dungeon chests/spawners is not stored; it is re-derived from the seed each load via `collectDungeonSites` (`lib/world/generation.ts`). Handled by `serializeLootedChests` / `readLootedChests` in `lib/game/save.ts`\n\nStored in localStorage under `SAVE_KEY` (`minecraft_save_v6`, defined in `lib/game/config.ts`)."

Pre-dungeon saves are therefore discarded in practice; the migration keeps the version chain complete regardless. `readSave` chains v1 → v2 → v3 → v4 → v5.\n- **v4** — adds optional `blockEntities` (chest contents). `migrateSaveV3toV4` is a pure version bump (absent → restores as no containers), so **`SAVE_KEY` was unchanged** at the time (player-placed chests only; worldgen and the index formula were untouched) and v1/v2/v3 saves load fine."
